**Sub lesson map (added 2026-11-08, whole-arc rule):** the
rows above already stocked the size climb; the text now
teaches it as Sub lesson 4, carried by the Saudi Arabia
Grade 4 rows (two-digit × two-digit by partial products),
the Pakistan Grade 4 row (partial products on the grid —
4-digit × 1-digit, 5-digit × 2-digit and 5-digit × 3-digit),
the Ethiopia rows (Grade 3: multiplying to 10,000; Grade 4:
to 1,000,000 with 2-digit and larger multipliers) and the
Rwanda Primary 6 row (numbers beyond 1,000,000 by a
multi-digit multiplier using partial products). No existing
row changed.

**Acceptance count: taught in 65 countries (Kazakhstan, Egypt, Greece, Libya, Jordan, Armenia, Vietnam, Laos, Yemen, Indonesia, Nicaragua, Ethiopia, South Sudan, Iraq, Bangladesh, Nepal, Cambodia, Kuwait, North Macedonia, Iran, China, Pakistan, Syria, Saudi Arabia, Rwanda, Sudan, India, Mongolia, Venezuela, Afghanistan, Uzbekistan, Ecuador, Albania, Bolivia, Tajikistan, Turkey, Tanzania, South Africa, Sierra Leone, Mauritania, El Salvador, Cyprus, Colombia, Angola, Moldova, Australia, Bhutan, Cabo Verde, Costa Rica, Cuba, Bulgaria, Eritrea, Chile, Dominican Republic, Guyana, Fiji, Honduras, Iceland, Peru, Suriname, Papua New Guinea, Sweden, Timor-Leste, Uganda, Turkmenistan)**.**. Kazakhstan carries the tables in order — 2 to 5 in Grade 2, 6 to 9 in Grade 3 — and multiplication by 10 and 100. Egypt carries multiplication from arrays as repeated addition in Grade 2 Term 2 (old Discover series — rows and columns counted, no × sign yet), through the properties of multiplication and multiplying by 10 and 0 in Primary 3's new Japanese-collaboration book and the associative and distributive properties in the old Term 2 book, to vertical multiplication, 2-digit and 3-digit by 1-digit, with multiplying by tens and hundreds, in Grade 3 (the Primary 3 rows were completed by the 2026-11-08 re-verification sweep). Greece carries the tables from Grade 2 and multiplication with division in Grade 3. Libya carries column multiplication with regrouping to hundreds in Grade 3. Jordan carries the tables for 2 to 5 in Grade 3. Armenia carries finding the unknown factor in Grade 4. Vietnam carries multiplication within 1000 in Grade 3. Other read countries teach multiplying inside their own Grade 2–4 books and join by merge.** Laos carries multiplication as repeated addition with tables ×2–×9 in the new Grade 2 book (to ×10 in the old guide) and multiplication with carrying, including by 10 and 100, in Grade 3. Yemen carries multiplication from repeated addition and the ×2, ×3, ×4, ×5 facts in Grade 2, through the ×6–×9 facts and multiplying by 10, 100 and 1000, to three-digit by three-digit multiplication with estimating by rounding. Indonesia carries multiplication from repeated addition to 100 in Grade 3 and column multiplication to 100 in Grade 4, through fraction multiplication by natural numbers in Grade 6, to integer, fraction and decimal multiplication in Grade 7 (Merdeka and K13 Semester 1). Grades 1–2 teach no multiplication, as the filing states. Nicaragua carries multiplication from the tables in Grade 2 (2, 5, 3, 4, 6, 7, 8, 9 in the old book; 5, 2, 3, 4, then 6, 7, 8, 9 in the 2025 book), through vertical multiplication in Grade 3 and 12 × 23 with the named properties in Grade 4, to decimal and fraction multiplication in Grades 5–6 and signed-number multiplication in Grade 7. One book error is never taught as fact: the old Grade 3 teacher guide's multiplication-tables page misprints '1 x 10 = 80' — the true value is 8 x 10 = 80. Ethiopia carries multiplication from the tables ×2, ×10, ×5, ×0/×1, ×3, ×4, ×6, ×7 and ×8 in Grade 2 — there is no ×9 section, ×9 appearing only in the full 0–10 table — through multiplying to 1,000 in Grade 3 and to 10,000, to multiplying to 1,000,000 with 2-digit and larger multipliers in Grade 4. South Sudan carries multiplication only in part — multiplication as repeated addition and groups with the 1–10 table and word problems in Grade 2, and multiplication of fractions in Secondary 1. Missing: column and multi-digit multiplication; no 2-digit × 1-digit is taught anywhere in the set. Iraq carries multiplication from repeated addition to 5×5 in Grade 2, through ×0–×10 and 2-digit × 1-digit without carrying in Grade 3, to 2-digit × 2-digit in Grade 4, 3-digit × 2-digit in Grade 5, fraction and decimal multiplication in Grade 6, and multiplying algebraic terms and expressions in Grades 7–8. Bangladesh carries multiplication from the tables of 1–10 with commutativity in Class 2, through tables of 13, 14 and 15 with 2-digit × 2-digit and 3-digit × 2-digit in Class 3, to 3-digit × 3-digit in Class 4, short methods in Class 5, multiplication on the number line in Class 6, and multiplication of exponents in Class 7. Two book errors are never taught as fact: the Class 3 tables print 6 × 9 as 55 (correct 54, printed p. 39) and 2 × 9 as 19 (correct 18, printed p. 45) — teach the correct values. Nepal carries multiplication from repeated addition with the ×2–×5 tables in Grade 1 (teach the p. 82 butterflies corrected: 4 × 4 = 16), through the ×6–×10 tables in Grade 2 (both books) and ×1–×12 with 2-digit × 2-digit and 3-digit × 2-digit in Grade 3, to 3–4 digit by up to 3-digit multiplication in Grade 4, and multiplication of fractions and decimals in Grades 5–6. Cambodia carries multiplication whole from repeated addition in Grade 2 through decimals, fractions and rationals in Grades 5–8. Kuwait carries multiplication from the concept itself in Grade 2 (repeated addition, arrays, ×2–×5), through the full ×1–×9 tables and 1-digit × 2-/3-digit work in Grade 3 and 2-digit × 2-or-more-digit work in Grade 4, to multiplying by one- and two-digit numbers in Grade 5. North Macedonia touches multiplication only at its very start — doubling to 10 at Grade 1 (2022), as readiness for multiplying by 2. Iran seeds the multiplication tables with skip-counting charts in Grade 1, builds multiplication in Grade 3, and reaches two-digit by two-digit multiplication in Grade 4.

Ghana also contributes to this lesson as curriculum, teacher and student-workbook (task-sheet) evidence only — Ghana's set holds no pupil textbook — and Ghana is not counted in the taught-in figure above. (Ghana rows stocked by the 2026-11-08 re-verification sweep.)

China carries multiplication from repeated-addition groundwork in Grade 1 and the table facts in Grade 2 to written two-digit × two-digit in Grade 3 and the laws and part-relations in Grade 4.

Pakistan (Punjab) carries multiplication from Grade 2's repeated addition and the 2, 3, 4, 5 and 10 tables through Classes 3–5's written methods to integer multiplication and its sign rules in Class 6.

Syria introduces multiplication in Grade 2 Semester 2 as joining equal groups and reads its commutativity off arrays read by rows and by columns; builds the tables of 2–9 one at a time in Grade 3 with their patterns; carries in stages through Grade 4 with partial products; and uses the commutative, associative and distributive properties as mental-calculation tools in Grades 5–6, before rational multiplication in Grade 7. Two review slips in the Grade 2 book are taught corrected where they occur: the printed 8 × 3 = 2 (the product is 24) and the apple labelled 16 whose three offered multiplications give 8, 12 and 18.

Saudi Arabia teaches the whole multiplication table inside Grade 3 — meaning, arrays and commutativity, the facts by pattern, the zero/one and associative properties — then builds multi-digit multiplication in Grade 4 on partial products and the area model, estimating first at every step.

Rwanda teaches multiplication table by table — ×2 to ×6 with two-digit multiplication and word problems in Year 2, the ×7, ×8 and ×9 tables with 3-digit × 2-digit multiplication and multiplying by 100 and 1,000 in Year 3, the table to 12 × 12 and quick multiplication by 5 in P4, quick methods and multiplication by a 3-digit number in P5, multiplication beyond 1,000,000 in P6, and multiplication of natural numbers and of integers in Senior 1.

Sudan builds the tables one at a time — 2, 10, 3, 4, 5, 6 in Grade 2; 7, 8, 9 in Grade 3, with multiplying by 0 and 1, by 10, 100 and multiples of ten, and two-digit and three-digit products — and returns to mental multiplication in Grade 9.

India carries multiplication through its NCERT ladder — Math-Magic Class 3–5 and Mathematics Class 6–7 — including skip counting on a number line; multiplying by thousands; cost tables; word problems.


Mongolia enters through its ministry textbooks Математик I, II, III, IV, V (Grades 1, 2, 3, 4, 5): multiplication from repeated addition and counting legs, heads, ears and eyes, through the whole table course of Grade 2 — each table built, practised and used in word problems — to written multiplication by two-digit numbers and the mental shortcuts (the 9-finger trick, ×25 and ×50, ×111).

Venezuela enters through the Colección Bicentenario, the MPPE's one-book-per-grade course: *Triángulos, rectángulos y algo más...* (Grade 2), *Aventuras de Patacalientes* (Grade 3), *Contando con los recursos* (Grade 4) and *Matemática Para La Vida* (Grade 7). From its books: from Grade 2: multiplication as repeated addition — buying dulces: Bs. 3 × 2 = Bs. 6; 8 bolsas × 2 = 16 from 2+2+2+2+2+2+2+2; the × sign read "veces" / "por"; from Grade 4: multiplication as repeated addition — 7 × Bs. 3 = 21 by repeated addition; 7 × 16 = 112 and the saving 112 − 21 = 91; from Grade 7: area model — Two-digit multiplication by geometric decomposition: 28 × 8 = 224 by splitting 28 = 20 + 8 and 8 = 5 + 3 into a rectangle of partial products.

Afghanistan enters through its Ministry of Education course — the Grade 1 course in two language editions (Pashto and Dari, one work with identical page maps) and Dari student books for Grades 4, 6, 7, 8, 10, 11 and 12, all printing year 1398 HS except the Grade 8 book, which prints 1399 HS on its cover and title page and 1398 HS on its specifications page. From its books: from Grade 4: review: multiplication (Lesson 5); multiplying a 2-digit number by a 2-digit number (Lesson 13); multiplying a 2-digit number by a 3-digit number (Lesson 14); multiplying a 2-digit number by a 4-digit number (Lesson 15); multiplying a 2-digit number by a multi-digit number (Lesson 16); checking a multiplication (Lesson 20).

Albania enters through its Mediaprint teacher's books (Libër Mësuesi, in Albanian) — TEACHER EVIDENCE only: no pupil's book is in hand for any grade, so Albania is never cited as a pupil-textbook source; what the teacher's books evidence is what is taught and in what order, through their lesson plans, worked answers and answer keys. From its books: from Grade 1 (teacher's book): Multiplication and division: multiplication and division are inverse operations — 4 × 5 = 20 leads to 20 ÷ 4…; Chapter opener, Diskuto: bricks and studs as arrays — write 1 × 1 = 1 and 1 × 2 = 2; the same brick total…; Grouping: split sweets into two equal groups in different arrays (one answer 1 × 4, another 2 × 2; 8 sweets…; Praktiko: grouping in twos then fives; repeated addition 2 + 2 + 2 + 2 + 2, 5 + 5, 3 + 3 + 3 + 3 + 3 turned…; Combined sheet: Mrs Mira's animals — 5 cats with 4 legs each: write 4 under each cat, 4 + 4 + 4 + 4 + 4 = 20,…. from Grade 5 (teacher's book): Meaning of multiplication; multiplication strategies and properties (commutative, associative, ×0, ×1); Multiplying by 10, 100, 1 000; 2-digit × 1-digit; 3-digit × 1-digit; 2-digit × 2-digit; Doubling numbers; quick methods ×9, ×11, ×19, ×21 (worked example 13 × 19 misprinted 547 for 247 — see band…. from Grade 6 (teacher's book): Derived facts (19 × 20 = 380 → 1.9 × 20 = 38; 0.5 × 0.8 = 0.4) (plans 37–38); : properties of multiplication and division (plans 39–40); : doubling and halving (plan 41); Opener + 5A: written multiplication of 2-, 3- and 4-digit numbers (grid/lattice method, 216 × 52 = 11 232;….

Bolivia enters through the Área Matemática sections of its Ministerio de Educación (Estado Plurinacional de Bolivia) Textos de Aprendizaje — the student books for Grades 7, 8, 10 and 12, each the maths section of that year's integrated year book, keeping the whole book's printed foliation, with no imprint or ISBN printed in any of them — and through the Compendio para Maestras y Maestros 2023–2024, the ministry's teacher compilation of the official student texts for all six Secundaria years (Grades 7–12), cited as teacher-compilation evidence only, never as or in place of a pupil text; its Secundaria 3 and Secundaria 5 sections are the only in-hand source for Grades 9 and 11, whose student texts are not in the record. From its books: from Grade 7: Multiplicación y división de enteros; from the Compendium's Secundaria 1 section (Grade 7): Los números enteros (Z).

Tajikistan enters through its Ministry-approved pupil's textbooks in Tajik (Cyrillic), all published in Dushanbe — Математика for Grade 5 (Маориф, 2022) and Grade 6 (5th edition, Маориф, 2021), Алгебра for Grades 7 (4th improved edition, 2023) and 8 (4th edition, 2023), and Геометрия — the continuation of stereometry — for Grade 11 (3rd edition, Лавҳ, 2020). From its books: from Grade 5: Multiplication as a sum of equal addends; Column multiplication of multi-digit numbers, justified by the distributive law.

Barbados enters as CURRICULUM EVIDENCE only, through the *Primary Mathematics Syllabus* (Classes 1–4) of its Ministry of Education and Human Resource Development — its Class 1 syllabus requires recalling multiplication facts up to 50, building and using the tables 2, 3, 4, 5 and 10, and multiplying up to 2-digit numbers by them; Class 2 adds the tables 6, 7, 8, 9 and 10 and multiplying numbers up to 9,999 by them and by multiples of 10; Class 3 requires the tables up to 12 × 12 in the body; Class 4 requires multiplying whole numbers by one-, two- and three-digit numbers. A syllabus states what is to be taught, in what order and to what depth; it teaches nothing itself. Barbados publishes no government pupil textbooks — primary mathematics books are commercial titles parents buy, and the syllabi's Suggested Texts appendices name them (*Caribbean Primary Mathematics* Levels 1–6; *Nelson Primary Maths for Caribbean Schools* 1–4; *Steps To Common Entrance Mathematics* 1–3) as named by the syllabus only: none is in hand and none is cited. Barbados is therefore named in this lesson's countries line and its syllabus requirements are cited in the table as curriculum evidence, but Barbados is not counted in the taught-in figure.

Turkey enters through the pupil's textbooks of its Millî Eğitim Bakanlığı (MEB — Ministry of National Education), all in Turkish, first volumes (1. Kitap) only — Grade 3 (ISBN 978-975-11-9897-6, TTKB approval 13/07/2026), Grade 6 (ISBN 978-975-11-8773-4, TTKB approval 30.06.2025) and Grade 7 (ISBN 978-975-11-9899-0, TTKB approval 1.07.2026); no edition, printing or year line is printed in any of the three books, and the grades' second volumes (2. Kitap) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. From its books: from Grade 7: Multiplication of integers built from repeated addition in a pattern table (air-conditioner buttons changing temperature by −2 °C); Sign rules for integer multiplication inferred by continuing the pattern to negative × negative.

Tanzania enters through the pupil's book of its Tanzania Institute of Education (TIE) — *Mathematics Standard Five — Pupil's Book*, the English-medium Standard Five book (© TIE 2019, published 2019, reprinted 2021; ISBN 978-9987-09-109-6; Certificate of Approval No. 282, approved by the Ministry of Education, Science and Technology on 5 December 2019, prepared to the 2016 primary Mathematics Syllabus). It is the only Tanzania mathematics book in hand: the other 20 recorded works — among them the Swahili-medium Hisabati Std 5, a separate book from this English one — are waiting-for-file and are cited nowhere. From its book: from Standard Five: products of whole numbers capped at six digits; up to 4-digit × up to 2-digit by place-value partial products, horizontal and vertical; 5-digit × 1- and 2-digit; multipliers up to three digits; word problems of crates × bottles per crate.

Armenia's stock in this lesson is widened by the full re-verification sweep of 2026-11-08, which re-read Armenia's maths books page by page and rebuilt the placements from the PDFs. Newly stocked from its Mathematics 3, Part 1 (student); Mathematics 4 (student); Mathematics 2, Part I (student); Mathematics 5 (student): multiplication by a one-digit number (first treatment); multiplying by 10, 100, 1000; multiplying and dividing round numbers; multiplying multi-digit numbers by a one-digit number; writing multiplication "in a column"; multiplying a multi-digit number by a two- and three-digit number; multiplying one-digit numbers; multiplying numbers; multiplying two-digit and one-digit numbers; multiplication of natural numbers; commutative and associative laws; column multiplication of multi-digit numbers.

## Morocco added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Morocco's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 books, all of them teacher guides or teacher editions (Dar Attakafa, Casablanca); 0 pupil (student) books** — and placed 610 teaching items. No Moroccan pupil maths textbook is in hand: the pupil's books are commercial print, and the library's systematic free shelf is Dar Attakafa's teacher-guide library. Every Morocco item in this section is therefore TEACHER EVIDENCE — a guide states the pupil book's lesson, its content and its order — cited as the evidence kind it is; Morocco is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until Moroccan pupil books are in hand and read. Set reconciliation, as the placements prove it: the 32 are distinct works (Le Monde des Maths ×6, L'Explicite en Maths ×6, Al Moufid primary ×3, Al Marjie primary ×2, Al Moufid collège ×3, Switch On! guides ×6, Switch On! Every Day booklets ×6; French 27 books, Arabic 5); textbooks.tsv carries only 28 of the 32 — a library-record gap, reported here and not edited (merges never edit the records). The primary guides all teach the one official revised programme (July 2020) — 32 lessons per grade, Grades 1–6, stated independently by up to four series per grade; the collège guides give the BIOF programme distributions for Grades 7–9. Read depth, as the placements declare it: the Arabic guides' legacy font encoding makes pdftotext return mojibake, so their contents were read from rendered pages; Books 6, 7 and 25 place their own year's programme distribution only (their readings of the other collège years are excluded, so nothing is double-counted); Book 31 places the distinct rows of its yearly programme table, a coarser grain than the lesson-by-lesson guides. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct lesson/topic the guide teaches the teacher to deliver, and its named exclusions — method/framework chapters, evaluation/bilan/support apparatus and the Switch On confirmation tests, Switch On 1's graphisme weeks, bibliographies, indexes and recording tables — are stocked nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 43 teacher-evidence items from 19 of the guides (Book 2, Book 5, Book 10, Book 11, Book 12, Book 13, Book 16, Book 17, Book 19, Book 21, Book 23, Book 24, Book 26, Book 27, Book 28, Book 29, Book 30, Book 31, Book 32; Grades 2, 3, 4, 5, 6).

**Book 2:** *Le Monde des Maths 2, Guide de l'enseignant(e), 2e année primaire* (teacher guide, Grade 2, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 9,555,550 bytes. Same collection and July-2020 revised programme as Book 1; items are its 32 numbered lessons — printed pp. from its Table des matières where the sommaire prints them, later lessons cited by sequence (seq.). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 5:** *L'Explicite en Maths CE1, Guide pédagogique* (CE1 = Grade 2) (teacher guide, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 18,580,797 bytes. Items are its 32 lesson sheets, printed pp. from its Sommaire — the Grade 2 programme corresponding to Book 2. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 10:** *المرجع في الرياضيات (Al Marjie fi al-Riyadiyat), دليل الأستاذة والأستاذ* (teacher guide, Grade 3, Arabic) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 18,309,585 bytes, 351 PDF pages. Its contents (read from rendered pages — legacy font encoding) list 32 numbered lessons in 6 units, the Grade 3 programme. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

Items are the distinct drill strands in its Sommaire (weeks 1–34); the confirmation tests are excluded in the placements as assessment apparatus. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 12:** *Switch On! 3e année, Guide de l'enseignant* (teacher guide, Grade 3, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 3,536,222 bytes, 88 PDF pages. Items are the distinct drill strands in its Sommaire; confirmation tests excluded in the placements. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 13:** *Switch On! 4e année, Guide de l'enseignant* (teacher guide, Grade 4, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 5,239,501 bytes, 88 PDF pages. Items are the distinct drill strands in its Sommaire; confirmation tests excluded in the placements. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 16:** *Switch On! Every Day CM1, livret d'activités rituelles* (Grade 4, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 3,704,467 bytes, 109 PDF pages. Same series and standing as Book 9. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 19:** *المفيد في الرياضيات (Al Moufid fi al-Riyadiyat), دليل الأستاذة والأستاذ* (teacher guide, Grade 5, Arabic) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 70,552,470 bytes, 330 PDF pages; new edition September 2020, ministry approval serial 515220 (3 Aug 2020). Its contents list 32 numbered lessons in 6 units, read from rendered pages (legacy font encoding). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 21:** *Switch On! 5e année, Guide de l'enseignant* (teacher guide, Grade 5, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 7,827,643 bytes, 88 PDF pages. Its Sommaire is a scanned table, read from the rendered page; items are its distinct drill strands; confirmation tests excluded in the placements. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 23:** *Le Monde des Maths 6, Guide de l'enseignant(e), 6e année primaire* (teacher guide, Grade 6, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 7,986,682 bytes, 300 PDF pages. Items are its 32 numbered lessons, printed pp. from its Table des matières. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 24:** *L'Explicite en Maths 6e, Guide pédagogique* (Grade 6) (teacher guide, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 8,497,260 bytes, 120 PDF pages. Items are its 32 lesson sheets, printed pp. from its Sommaire — the Grade 6 programme, corresponding to Book 23 with the series' own additions (an interest sheet; a powers sheet). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 26:** *Le Monde des Maths 4, Guide de l'enseignant(e), 4e année primaire* (teacher guide, Grade 4, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 4,953,260 bytes, 190 PDF pages; read in place from its staging copy (never deleted from staging). Items are its 32 numbered lessons — printed pp. from its Table des matières where printed, later units by sequence. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 27:** *Le Monde des Maths 3, Guide de l'enseignant(e), 3e année primaire* (teacher guide, Grade 3, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 6,204,899 bytes. Items are its 32 numbered lessons — printed pp. from its Table des matières where printed, later units by sequence; the Grade 3 programme, corresponding to Book 10. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 28:** *L'Explicite en Maths CE2, Guide pédagogique* (CE2 = Grade 3) (teacher guide, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 8,578,274 bytes. Items are its 32 lesson sheets, printed pp. from its Sommaire — the Grade 3 programme, corresponding to Books 10 and 27. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 29:** *L'Explicite en Maths CM1, Guide pédagogique* (CM1 = Grade 4) (teacher guide, French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 8,896,704 bytes. Items are its 32 lesson sheets, printed pp. from its Sommaire — the Grade 4 programme, corresponding to Books 26 and 30. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 30:** *المفيد في الرياضيات (Al Moufid fi al-Riyadiyat), دليل الأستاذة والأستاذ* (teacher guide, Grade 4, Arabic; contents bilingual Arabic/French) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 21,206,485 bytes, 357 PDF pages; 2019 edition. Its contents list the same 32 lessons as Books 26 and 29, with bilingual titles, read from rendered pages. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 31:** *المرجع في الرياضيات (Al Marjie fi al-Riyadiyat), دليل الأستاذ(ة)* (teacher guide, Grade 2, Arabic) — Morocco, Dar Attakafa (Casablanca); 55,959,139 bytes, 393 PDF pages. Items are the distinct rows of its yearly programme table (guide printed pp. 48–50, read from rendered pages), which states the Grade 2 content by domain with learning objectives — a coarser grain than the lesson-by-lesson guides, as the placements declare. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

## El Salvador added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read El Salvador's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — the MINEDUCYT **ESMATE** programme (Proyecto de Mejoramiento de los Aprendizajes en Matemática en Educación Básica y Educación Media, MINEDUCYT–JICA, 2015–2019): **63 filed entries = 43 distinct works** — 16 student files covering the 11 grade textbooks (Grades 1–11; Grade 1 in 4 part-files, Grade 2 in 3), 25 teacher guides, and 2 JICA materials documents (the materials-use guideline and the Japan–El Salvador unit list) filed 11 times each, byte-identical, MD5-proved — the 2 documents are programme apparatus with no teaching content, cited once each in the country record and stocked in no lesson. The read placed 383 teaching items: 281 from the student textbooks and 102 teacher-evidence items from the guides (one per unit per grade). In the sections below the student books are cited as Books S1–S13 and the guides as Books T1–T12, in grade order. El Salvador COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its student-textbook rows; its guide rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — the guides reproduce the student lessons with teaching plans, worked solutions and a unit test per unit — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count. Book errors: none that changes a placement (the Grade 9 book's history opener dates al-Khwārizmī to c. 825 BC; he lived c. 780–850 AD — a front-matter error in an excluded motivation essay, in no teaching item, so nothing is taught from it). The placements' named exclusions — *Recortables* cut-out pages, covers, credits and unit-opener history/motivation essays, the GeoGebra practice lessons of Grades 10–11, end-of-unit and end-of-trimester tests in the student books, teacher-guide front matter, and the Grade 6 book's *Repaso* review sections — are stocked nowhere.

Into this lesson the placements stock 18 items (13 from student books, 5 teacher evidence) from Book S2, Book T2, Book S4, Book T3, Book S5, Book T4, Book S6, Book T5.

## Cyprus added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cyprus's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **75 distinct works: 22 pupil textbooks (primary Grades 1–6, part-books) and 53 teacher guides (Οδηγός Εκπαιδευτικού), all Cyprus Ministry of Education (Υπουργείο Παιδείας), in Greek** — and placed 281 teaching items: 228 from the pupil books and 53 teacher-evidence items (one per guide, worded from the guide's stated success indicators, ΔΕΙΚΤΕΣ ΕΠΙΤΥΧΙΑΣ). SET CORRECTION: the expansion map recorded Cyprus Maths as 22 student + 43 teacher = 65 in hand; the records' union proves 22 student + 53 teacher = **75** (staging books.json carries 64 files = 22 student + 42 teacher, all secured in GitHub; textbooks.tsv adds 11 further teacher guides held as git-blob files that are in no books.json: Grade 1 Units 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11, Grade 2 Unit 1, Grade 4 Unit 1) — the correction is recorded here; the library records themselves are not edited (merges never edit the records). In the sections below the pupil books are cited as Books S1–S22 and the guides as Books T1–T53, in grade order. Cyprus COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its pupil-book rows; its guide rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — a guide states the pupil book's unit, its indicators and its lesson organisation — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). The read's three flagged claims are carried exactly: (1) the map's “65” is corrected above; (2) the Grade 3 Unit 1 teacher guide's indicator Αρ2.1 states quantities “to 10,000” where its pupil book's Unit 1 teaches to 1,000 — the guide's figure is NOT confirmed by the pupil book and is worded as the guide's own claim in that guide's row, never as pupil teaching; (3) three guides carry the books' own unit-number mislabels — the Grade 4 Unit 3 guide's contents header reads “ΕΝΟΤΗΤΑ 2”, the Grade 6 Unit 3 guide's running head reads “ΕΝΟΤΗΤΑ 2”, the Grade 6 Unit 9 guide's running head reads “ΕΝΟΤΗΤΑ 8” — the contents match their cover units, so the rows record the true units with the errors noted in the book entries, taught corrected, never as printed. Production date stamps inside three files (Grade 5 Part 1 “22/06/2026”, Grade 6 Part 1's InDesign stamp 05/03/2026, Grade 6 Part 3 “01/07/2026”) are PDF production stamps in the ministry files as held, not edition dates. The placements' named exclusions — front matter, icon keys, contents pages, cut-out card appendices (“Για τη σελίδα …”), Διαμορφωτική Αξιολόγηση (formative assessment) pages and enrichment sets repeating unit content — are stocked nowhere; the apparent “330C/20C” in the Grade 3 Part 1 enrichment is the degree sign merging with the digits in the text layer (33 °C falling by 2 °C), not a book error. Teacher-guide coverage in hand: Grades 1–3 Units 1–12 complete, Grade 4 Units 1–3 only, Grade 5 Units 1–4, Grade 6 Units 1–10; the further guides that exist only as ministry hub links (Grade 4 Units 4+, Grade 5 Units 5+, Grade 6 Units 11+) are not in hand, not read and not counted. Cyprus's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (75/75) has been read and placed; if the hub-link guides are later secured, Cyprus reverts to PARTIAL until they are read.

Into this lesson the placements stock 22 items (16 from pupil books, 6 teacher evidence) from Book S4, Book S5, Book S6, Book S8, Book S11, Book S12, Book S14, Book S16, Book S17, Book S18, Book T12, Book T15, Book T18, Book T21, Book T22, Book T27.

## Sri Lanka added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sri Lanka's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs in two passes — **94 files / 66 distinct works: 26 pupil textbooks and 40 teacher guides, all Sri Lanka Educational Publications Department (EPD) / National Institute of Education (NIE), in the country's three media — English, Sinhala and Tamil** — and placed 479 items: 289 from the pupil books and 190 teacher-evidence items (the secondary guides at one item per term of the guide's own lesson sequence; the primary guides at one item per syllabus competency group or per part). Pass 1 read the expansion map's 59 files (32 works); Pass 2 pinned 35 further in-hand files the map's subject filter had missed — 34 NIE teacher guides for Grades 1–13 and one pupil textbook (Book S26, filed under the subject "Science for Technology") — and read every one. In the sections below the pupil books are cited as Books S1–S26 and the guides as Books T1–T40. Sri Lanka COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil-book rows; its guide rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — a guide states its syllabus's competencies, its lesson sequence and its teaching instructions — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). Set facts carried exactly: (1) the Grade 9 Sinhala pupil book is held only as chapter files of two different printings (Books S9–S10) whose shared chapter 20 is a different chapter in each, and NO chapter-26 file exists in either split — the English twin's chapter 26 is Algebraic fractions; the hole is carried as a set gap, not as read; (2) the 2018 Sinhala Grade 9 teacher guide is filed twice (two scans of one work) and is placed once (Book T2), and the file held as the Grade 7 2008 manual's "Tamil" copy is byte-identical to the English manual (same git blob SHA) and is cited once, as the English manual (Book T10); (3) the year-less Tamil Grade 4 and Grade 5 guides are proved by byte size to be NIE's 2015 editions, not the far larger 2019/2020 scans NIE also lists (Books T39–T40); (4) the 1976 Grade 12 Mathematics Part I scan (Book S23) ends with some six pages of an unrelated German textbook's verb tables (PDF pp. 271–276) — its mathematics runs to PDF p. 270 and its last chapters were sampled, not contents-verified; (5) the Grade 1 Sinhala 2026 guide is in hand as a Term 1 volume only (Book T33) — its Terms 2–3 are stocked nowhere — and the Grade 1 Tamil 2016 guide (Book T31) is marked DRAFT on its cover and is placed as filed; (6) the Grade 11 guide's Term 2 table labels one lesson (competency 23.6, inside the geometry cluster) "Central limit theorem" — recorded as printed in Book T19's entry; no row in this course rests on that label; (7) Grade 10 Mathematics Part I (English) and Grade 7 Mathematics Part I (Tamil, 2007–2015 edition) are NOT in hand in any filing — library gaps, not unread books; their twins in the other medium are held and read (Books S17 and S13/S14). The placements' named exclusions — revision exercises, answers, glossaries, assessment apparatus, and the guides' specimen lesson plans and school-based assessment instruments beyond their sequences — are stocked nowhere. Sri Lanka's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (94/94 files) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

## Colombia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Colombia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **27 distinct works: 26 student books and 1 teacher guide, all Colombia Ministerio de Educación Nacional (MEN), in Spanish** — Escuela Nueva Matemáticas cartillas Grades 1–5 (11), Nivelemos Matemáticas student workbooks Grades 1–4 (4), Aulas sin fronteras Grade 6 Unidad 1 student book and its teacher guide (2), Postprimaria student guides Grades 6–9 (4), Secundaria Activa student modules Grades 6–9 (4), and Vamos a Aprender Matemáticas 10 and 11 (2) — 3,928 PDF pages — and placed 293 teaching items: 288 from the student books and 5 teacher-evidence items from the one teacher guide (Book 23). SET CORRECTIONS carried exactly: (1) the expansion map's “28 student + 1 teacher = 29” over-counts by 2 — textbooks.tsv carries duplicate records of Escuela Nueva Grado 1 Cartilla 1 and Grado 2 Cartilla 1 (old blob/main rows for the same two works that also landed as release assets); the distinct set is 27 works and the duplicates are cited once (the library records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records); (2) the set in hand contains NO Grade 3 Cartilla 3 — Grade 3 holds Cartillas 1–2 plus the Nivelemos Grade 3 workbook, and Cartilla 2 completes Unidades 6–9; nothing in hand is a mislabelled Cartilla 3, and no never-published ruling is needed or made; (3) Nivelemos Matemáticas Grado 1 (Book 10) has NO asset in any GitHub release — it was read from the MEN source file itself, landed at exactly the census size (5,020,415 bytes) with EOF intact; the library side still owes that file a GitHub landing. The two Vamos a Aprender books (Books 26–27) are image-only viewer assemblies with no text layer, read from rendered pages (covers, printed contents pp. 6–7 and body spot-checks — Matemáticas 10 printed p. 110 “Teorema del seno”, Matemáticas 11 printed p. 120 “Derivada de una función en un punto”); printed page = PDF page in the sampled range. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–27 in the placements' order. Colombia COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its student-book rows; Book 23's rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — the guide companions Book 15 with teaching plans, activity scripts and answers and teaches no student content itself — cited as the evidence kind it is and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). Book errors: none material at placement grain — the Secundaria Activa Grade 9 module's contents page misprints two of its own headings (“Captítulo”, “Undiad 2”); the content is unaffected and rows use the corrected unit names. The placements' named exclusions — presentation letters, “how this book works” pages, author/imprint pages, transversal civics/health/environment themes, practice/evaluation apparatus (“Practica más”, “Evaluación del aprendizaje”, answer keys) and the Escuela Nueva “Estándares/Competencias” boxes at each unidad opening (MEN's own curriculum-standard statements; the taught content is placed at the guías themselves) — are stocked nowhere. The Círculos de Aprendizaje integrated books and the Retos para Gigantes Transición weekly guides are NOT in this set (filed as integrado, not matematicas) and are stocked nowhere. Colombia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (27/27) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

## Andorra added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Andorra's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 documents, all of them official Escola Andorrana curriculum programmes published by decree in the Butlletí Oficial del Principat d'Andorra (BOPA); 0 pupil (student) books** — and placed 679 curriculum-requirement items. A programme states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself. Every Andorra item in this section is therefore TEACHER EVIDENCE — a curriculum requirement, worded as a requirement — cited as the evidence kind it is; Andorra is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until Andorran pupil books are in hand and read. Set correction, as the placements prove it: the expansion map recorded 3 books in hand; the true set is 4 — the grades 1–6 programme (Decret 376/2021, Annex VII) is held in the GitHub repo but sits in no staging books.json, which is why the map missed it. All four programmes are Andorra's OWN (Govern d'Andorra, in Catalan); the Spanish and French systems also taught in Andorra (Spain's Anaya and Innovamat manuals; France's Le Livre Scolaire) are list-only names on official school book lists — no file is held, so they are cited nowhere in this course and credited to no country — and the Escola Andorrana itself has no commercial pupil-textbook series, so programmes are all that exist to hold. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct stated requirement — each specific competency, and each distinct entry in the programmes' learning-resource tables, tagged by cycle where the table splits by cycle — and a procedure entry that merely restates a concept entry is folded into it, not double-counted. The placements' named exclusions — the decree preambles, the introductions and competency rationale, the didactic orientations, the assessment criteria and end-of-cycle expectations, the synthesis tables (verified verbatim repeats of the competency sections), the attitudes/values lists, Book 1's glossary and opening summary table, and the Council of Europe cross-references — are stocked nowhere. Book 3's synthesis table words its C2 end-of-cycle expectation as modelling "situacions cientificotècniques" (scientific-technical) inside the social-sciences programme — a copy-over slip in the decree as published; Book 3's competency section itself is consistently social-sciences, and the slip is copied into no row below. Book 2 states its cone/polyhedron sections twice (forms table and models table, printed pp. 32–33) — counted once in the placements and stocked once. Printed page = PDF page in all four books (Books 2–4 carry the BOPA folio; Book 1's Portal PDF is paginated at the foot the same way). Into this lesson the placements stock 7 teacher-evidence items from Book 1.

TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an official curriculum programme: it states what is required to be taught and teaches nothing itself.

## Angola added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Angola's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 books, all pupil textbooks: Matemática, Manuais da 1.ª–6.ª Classe (Ministério da Educação — MED, Angola; updated editions of 2021, in Portuguese). There is no teacher guide in the Angola Maths set in hand, so every Angola row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Angola counts in every lesson it stocks** — and placed 229 teaching items. The expansion map's “6 student + 0 teacher = 6” is correct: download-lists/angola.md, the phase3-staging checklist, textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json (“pushed”) and the staging books.json files all give the same six distinct pupil textbooks, and nothing else. None of the six PDFs was on local disk (pusher-secured only), so all six were fetched fresh for the read from the books' recorded second official source, the ministry's Xilonga host, byte-exact against the records. One set nuance: the portal's 32-manual set contains no 2.ª Classe Matemática at all — the 2.ª book in hand (Book 2) exists only as an unlinked Xilonga file (MAT02-LR.pdf) found by pattern probe on 2026-10-23. A Guia Prático Matemática 6ª exists only as an exact-link reader copy and is NOT in hand — cited nowhere; if one is ever secured it enters as teacher evidence only. The Book 2 and Book 4 PDFs carry a massively duplicated text layer (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the files; the content is fully legible and both books were read in full from it. Twelve book errors are recorded in the placements; they are carried in the book entries below and taught corrected. The placements' named exclusions — the apresentação (ministerial foreword letter) and anthem/flag/civic front matter, the bibliografia pages, pure colouring, tracing and copying practice with no new concept, the editorial production notes printed inside the books, and exercise sets as such (exercises practise the item under which they sit) — are stocked nowhere. Printed-page bases: Books 1 and 3 — printed page = PDF page − 1; Books 2, 4, 5 and 6 — printed page = PDF page. Into this lesson the placements stock 10 items (all from pupil textbooks) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 5 and Book 6.

**Book 1:** *Matemática — Manual da 1.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 1) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Editora Moderna, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-762-267-0; 4,293,873 bytes, 81 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1).

**Book 2:** *Matemática — Manual da 2.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 2) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Mayamba Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-761-297-8; 3,437,647 bytes, 56 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book error, taught corrected: printed p. 23 calls the minuend “diminuendo ou aditivo” — “aditivo” is wrong for subtraction; the course teaches minuend. Editorial production notes are printed as lesson content on printed p. 50 — production matter, cited nowhere. The PDF's text layer is massively duplicated (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the file; the content is fully legible and the book was read in full from it.

**Book 3:** *Matemática — Manual da 3.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 3) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Progresso Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-762-270-0; 1,798,728 bytes, 85 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1). Book errors, taught corrected: printed p. 21 repeats the minuend misnomer “diminuendo ou aditivo” (teach: minuend); printed p. 36's decimal-multiplication worked example is internally inconsistent — it states 4,15 × 2,3, lays out 3,15 and prints 7,245 (correct: 4,15 × 2,3 = 9,545); the exercise under “Noção de circunferência” at printed p. 44 repeats the parallel-lines exercise verbatim (a copy-paste error in the book); printed p. 62 prints the false line “20 m = 200 cm” (correct: 20 m = 2 000 cm); printed p. 64's formula under decâmetro repeats “1 hm = 100 m” (correct: 1 dam = 10 m).

**Book 4:** *Matemática — Manual da 4.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 4) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Mayamba Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-761-296-1; 1,983,954 bytes, 112 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book error, taught corrected: printed p. 43 claims division “is only possible if the dividend is a multiple of the divisor” (exact division only) — contradicted by Book 3's own division-with-remainder teaching; the course teaches division with remainder. Editorial production notes are printed as page content on printed pp. 17, 18, 21, 24, 25, 28, 30 and 45 — production matter, cited nowhere; the Tema 3 heading is misprinted inside the book (at printed p. 102); PDF pp. 106–112 are blank/back matter apart from the bibliografia (printed p. 107), cited nowhere. The PDF's text layer is massively duplicated (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the file; the content is fully legible and the book was read in full from it.

**Book 5:** *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 5) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Texto Editores, 2021, 2.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-8884-84-8; 4,730,235 bytes, 128 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book errors, taught corrected: printed p. 29's worked example 12 + 37 + 7 + 3 is printed as = 55 with a garbled regrouping (correct total: 59); printed p. 73's equivalence line prints 1/3 = 2/6 = 2/9 (the last term should be 3/9); printed p. 127's median worked example uses an ordered list containing 12, which is not in the exercise's data, and drops one 10 (the median is taught from the data as printed in the exercise: ordered 6, 7, 9, 10, 10, 10, 11, 13, 14 → median 10).

**Book 6:** *Matemática — Manual da 6.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 6) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Texto Editores, 2021, 2.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-8884-85-5; 2,544,284 bytes, 104 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book notes, taught corrected: printed p. 51 first presents the ambiguous expression 28 : 7 × 2 with two candidate readings and tells the pupil to ask the teacher — the rule is fixed by the worked examples on printed p. 52, and the course teaches the standard order (× and ÷ in order, before + and −); printed p. 62's direct-proportionality worked line prints the third ratio with denominator 6 where the sequence's term is 5 (150/5 = 30 — taught from the sequence). The book carries printer's proof marks, and one running footer misprints “Matemática 5ª Classe” (printed p. 99) — production matter, cited nowhere.

## Bahamas added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Bahamas' whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 documents, all of them Bahamas Ministry of Education curriculum or examination-syllabus documents; 0 pupil (student) textbooks and 0 teacher guides** — and placed 496 requirement items. A curriculum states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself. Every Bahamas item in this section is therefore CURRICULUM EVIDENCE — a statement of what the Bahamas requires, worded as a requirement — cited as the evidence kind it is (Book 3's items are examination-SYLLABUS evidence, on the same standing); the Bahamas is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana/Barbados evidence-only precedent, until a Bahamas pupil book is in hand and read. Set correction, as the placements prove it: the expansion map recorded the in-hand set as “4 student + 0 teacher” — the count of 4 is right, the kind is wrong; all four documents are ministry requirement documents (kind = curriculum in both the staging books.json files and textbooks.tsv). The file the records title *BJC Mathematics Curriculum, Grades 7–9* is actually the *Bahamas Junior Certificate 044 Mathematics Syllabus 2003* — a 10-page scanned examination syllabus with no text layer, read visually page by page — and the held Senior High file is the July 2019 revision of the Grades 10–12 guidelines; both are placed as what they are. No pupil textbook or teacher guide is in hand: the pupil books Bahamas schools use are commercial (Hodder *Bahamas Primary Mathematics* Books 1–6 — existence verified in the records, no file held; Books 1–2 name Silver Burdett Ginn Mathematics and Harcourt Math in classroom use, and Book 4's bibliography names STP Caribbean Mathematics, Mathematics: A Complete Course, Fundamental Mathematics for the Caribbean and Nelson Caribbean Mathematics — named only, none held), and the one school-compiled pupil text with an exact link in the records (Akhepran International Academy, *Mathematics BJC Textbook — Grade 7*, 2023) was never downloaded and sits in no record set, so it is not part of this read and is cited nowhere. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct numbered objective in each guidelines book's Scope of Work body — the Content column read with every objective — Book 3 at one item per numbered topic row of its Topics table and Book 4 at one item per CONCEPTS/TOPICS block; an objective restated in full on a continuation page is counted once, at its first full statement, and the two requirements the documents restate inside a second strand (Book 1 Grade 2 estimation, item 69; Book 2 Grade 5 rounding of decimals, item 256) are stocked as the placements place them, each row naming the restatement. The placements' named exclusions — Benchmarks, Scope-and-Sequence charts, pacing guides and Essential Questions in Books 1–2; the Problems of the Day sets, Materials Lists, assessment rubrics, the Children's Literature list and References (record items R1–R7 and R9–R11); Book 3's examination administration, its 19 general aims and its erratum sheet (R8); Book 4's Scope and Sequence charts, Glossary and Technology Integration pages — are stocked nowhere. Book errors found in the read are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 18 requirement-evidence items from Book 1 and Book 2.

**Book 1:** *Primary School Mathematics Curriculum Guidelines, Grades 1–3* (curriculum guidelines) — Bahamas, Ministry of Education, Department of Education, September 2010; 5,558,181 bytes, 263 PDF pages; Scope of Work printed page = PDF page − 51. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — a curriculum states what is required to be taught; it teaches nothing itself.

CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — a curriculum states what is required to be taught; it teaches nothing itself.

## Belize added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Belize's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 documents, all of them Belize Ministry of Education *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide* books (August 2012) — Infant 1, Standard 1 and Standard IV; 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 108 teaching items. Every document in the set is a teacher's guide: it states the learning outcomes pupils must reach and teaches the teacher how to teach each one (comments, sample lesson objectives, specific content, sample activities and assessment). Every Belize item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — Belize's guides teach teachers how to teach the topic; no Belize pupil textbook is in hand — cited as the evidence kind it is; Belize is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Belize pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the pupil books on the official National Textbook Program list (*My BZ Math* Infant 1–Standard 4, Macmillan; *Caribbean Primary Math* Bk 6 for Standard 5, Hodder; *Active Math* for Standard 6, Macmillan) are all recorded missing in the library files, and the guides' own textbook linkages point to the legacy pupil series *Primary Mathematics for Belize* (Hodder, by term codes Y1T1…/S1T1…), which is named by the guides only — not in hand, not read, and not a course source. Three further Maths documents — the *Primary curriculum: Mathematics* division webs (Lower Division, Middle Division, Upper Division) — are recorded at exact link only, never downloaded and in no books.json; they are not part of this read and are cited nowhere. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct numbered learning outcome in each guide's Standards and Learning Outcomes section (35 outcomes in each guide, 105 in all) plus 3 items taught in a detailed section but stated in no outcome of that guide (items 64, 65 and 87, marked D); outcome numbering is continuous across the series (Infant 1 carries M1.1–M20.1, Standard 1 continues at M1.10, Standard IV at M1.16), which is why the codes do not restart at 1 in the later guides. Printed page = PDF page in all three guides. The placements' named exclusions — the guides' front matter (programme philosophy: goals, rationale, assessment discussion), strand introductions and Big Ideas prose, sample lesson objectives, sample assessments, sample activities and print/technology resource columns as apparatus, the shared manipulatives list, the websites lists, the shared Mathematics Glossary (carried in the placements as record item R2 — a teacher reference list, cited nowhere as content), and the standards each guide marks N/A at its level (Book 1: M2 place value and M13 equations; Book 2: M14 measurement concepts; Book 3: M5 position/ranking numbers and M14) — are stocked nowhere. The 14 book errors and inconsistencies found in the read (placements E1–E14) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: item 35 is placed as data interpretation, not probability, though the guide files it under its probability standard (E5); item 51's set language is taught with “complement” for the book's “compliment” (E6); item 66's many-to-one correspondence is taught as one symbol or unit standing for many objects, not the guide's function-style definition (E8); item 82 is stocked WITHOUT the outcome's fourth bullet (“expand powers with same base to add or subtract”), which is not a valid add/subtract procedure (E9); item 81 is placed as stated and flagged as vague and advanced for its level (E10); item 104 is placed as measurement and taught with the standard measurement meanings, the guide's own definition of precision being a data-management sense (E13); and item 106 follows the guide's own correct definitions — its assessment text's “medium” for median is an error (E12). Into this lesson the placements stock 3 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 2 and Book 3.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Standard 1* (teacher guide, Grade 3) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 803,827 bytes, 62 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–16; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 17–56. Printed page = PDF page. Outcome numbering continues the series from Book 1 (M1.10 onwards). TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

**Book 3:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Standard IV* (teacher guide, Grade 6) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 868,323 bytes, 51 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–15; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 16–45. Printed page = PDF page. Outcome numbering continues the series (M1.16 onwards). The detailed sections of this guide are markedly thinner than in Books 1–2 (several state “Specific Content: N/A”); placements rest on the outcomes as stated. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

Belize's class for Maths is PARTIAL (teacher-evidence-only): all 3 in-hand guides are read and stocked, but no pupil textbook is in hand, and the three *Primary curriculum: Mathematics* webs (Lower, Middle and Upper Division) sit at exact link only, unread.

## Australia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Australia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 books, all of them student textbooks** (Years 8–10: eight Cambridge volumes — the Essential Mathematics Australian Curriculum, Victorian Curriculum and CambridgeMATHS NSW lines, plus the GOLD support edition and the CORE pathway volume — and one Jacaranda volume, Maths Quest 10+10A) — and placed 844 teaching items. Australia has no government textbook series (ACARA writes the Australian Curriculum but publishes and prescribes no textbooks); the set is the school-used commercial series the country records name, and every book is cited as such. All 9 files are distinct works (distinct ISBNs, extents and contents, verified per book on the read); the publisher-line relations are stated in the placements: Book A1 (GOLD) is the support edition of Book A2's Year 8 material, Book A3 is the same line in its Victorian ©2024 edition, Books A4 and A6 are the NSW syllabus editions, and Book A9 is Book A8's CORE pathway companion. In the sections below the books are cited as Books A1–A9. Australia COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every item is pupil-textbook teaching. The read's four flagged claims are carried exactly: (F1) Book A3's contents lists its Chapter 11, Algorithmic thinking, as “(online only)”, but the file in hand contains the full chapter, bound after the Answers (printed pp. 926–941) — it is read and placed, and it founds this course's algorithmic-thinking lesson; (F2) Book A7's contents lists a Chapter 18, Algorithmic thinking, with no page numbers, and the chapter is NOT in the file (searched end to end; the book closes with its Glossary, printed p. 1106, and Index, printed p. 1117) — recorded as absent, not read, and no item rests on it; (F3) Book A9's running head at printed p. 644 prints section 10E's code as “0E” — the book's own production error; the placement cites 10E, as the section's heading and contents give it; (F4) only the 9 Maths books are this read's set — the country records' wider Australia picture (12 staged books across subjects, plus filled-by-exact-link titles) is not merged into this count, and in particular the two senior Maths titles that exist only as exact-link records (Cambridge Senior Specialist Mathematics, QCE Units 3&4, 2e, and Cambridge Senior Mathematical Methods, QCE Units 3&4, 2e) are NOT in hand and were NOT read. The placements' named exclusions — front matter and “how to use this book” guides; every chapter's pre-tests, progress quizzes, unlettered applications / problem-solving / modelling / investigation spreads, puzzles and games, chapter summaries, checklists and reviews, semester reviews, glossaries, indexes and answer sections — are apparatus/assessment, teach no new content, and are stocked nowhere. The set is Years 8–10 secondary only, so the primary lessons (counting, place value, first steps) receive no Australia item — that is the set's shape, not a reading gap. Australia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (9/9) has been read and placed; the two senior QCE exact-link titles do not block the in-hand class (if exact-link titles are ever ruled in-scope for the courses reckoning, Australia drops to PARTIAL until they are grabbed and read). Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items from Book A1, Book A2, Book A3.

**Book A1:** *Cambridge Mathematics 8 (CambridgeMaths GOLD)* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2012, ISBN 978-1-107-64383-3; 62,774,078 bytes, 554 PDF pp. The GOLD support edition of the Essential Mathematics Year 8 line, developed for students who require additional support in mathematics; a distinct work from Book A2 (different ISBN, extent and arrangement), not a duplicate file. Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 14).

**Book A2:** *Essential Mathematics for the Australian Curriculum Year 8, Third Edition* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2019, ISBN 978-1-108-77281-5; 35,833,730 bytes, 846 PDF pp. The main Year 8 Australian Curriculum volume of the Essential Mathematics line (Book A1 is its Gold support sibling). Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 18).

**Book A3:** *Essential Mathematics for the Victorian Curriculum Year 8, Third Edition* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2024, ISBN 978-1-009-48075-8; 24,871,876 bytes, 955 PDF pp. The Victorian Curriculum state edition of the Essential Mathematics Year 8 line — a distinct work from Book A2. Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 20). Its contents lists Chapter 11, Algorithmic thinking, as “(online only)”, but the full chapter IS bound into this file, after the Answers (printed pp. 926–941), and is read and placed (flag F1).

## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item (item 1).

**Book 1:** *Antología de Matemáticas — Nivel Sétimo* (student antología) — Costa Rica, Universidad Estatal a Distancia (UNED) / Colegio Nacional de Educación a Distancia (CONED), 2017; 6,973,434 bytes, 242 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page throughout. Pupil book (distance-secondary antología).

## Cuba added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cuba's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **29 in-hand entries = 25 distinct works (MINED, Editorial Pueblo y Educación, Cuba), every one read in full**: student textbooks for Grades 1 and 3–12, student workbooks for Grades 1, 2 and 3, and teacher documents (Programa and Orientaciones metodológicas) for Grades 1–5, 8 and 11 — and placed 247 teaching items (202 student-side, 45 teacher-evidence). **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 14 student + 8 teacher is WRONG** — the staging books.json holds 13 student + 9 teacher entries and textbooks.tsv holds 7 further secured entries; duplicated filings are cited once (the Grade 3 and Grade 8 student books were each verified in a second edition; the Grade 4 and Grade 5 student books are each filed twice as byte-identical copies — Grade 4 MD5 01c106741b186cf28d360854a40d7e93, Grade 5 MD5 b48e28b1df820e2451bb44eb33b5ac11). Record corrections the read proved, carried exactly: the record's “Matemática 2do grado Parte I student textbook” is in fact the COMPLETE Grade 2 workbook (Cuaderno de trabajo — Matemática 2, ISBN 978-959-13-1854-1) — **no Grade 2 student textbook is in hand**, and Grade 2 rests on its workbook, Programa and Orientaciones; the record's Grade 5 student byte size (36,868,096 B) is a truncated copy — the complete file read is 77,146,019 B / 396 pp; the Grade 11 teacher documents belong to the newer Perfeccionamiento programme and do **not** match the in-hand Grade 11 student book (2005 edition — its limits, derivatives and integrals chapters have no teacher companion in hand, and the programme's trigonometry unit has no chapter in that book), so they are filed as that programme's own teacher evidence, never as evidence for the 2005 book; the MINED portal source URLs now return soft-404 pages — the reads used Wayback captures of those same URLs and the GitHub routes. The placements' named exclusions — exercise sets, chapter exercises, self-evaluation sections, answers appendices, mathematical tables, historical feature essays and front/back matter — are stocked nowhere. Teacher items are TEACHER EVIDENCE — they state what the programme requires and how it is taught — are labelled as such on every row, and are never counted in this lesson's acceptance figure; student textbook and workbook items are COUNTED. Printed pages are the books' own printed folios as the placements cite them (each book's printed-page basis is stated in its book entry). Into this lesson the placements stock 14 items (items 5, 18, 22, 25, 30, 38, 47, 48, 58, 65, 68, 70, 76, 98): 9 student items and 5 teacher-evidence items.

**Book 1:** *Matemática 1er grado* (student textbook, Grade 1) — Cuba, MINED, Editorial Pueblo y Educación; Villalón Incháustegui et al., ©1988, 10th reprint 2013, ISBN 978-959-13-0341-7; 139 PDF pp; printed page = PDF page − 2; a picture book with no index — structure mapped by sampling the whole book in order. Pupil workbook. The library record's “Matemática 2do grado Parte I student textbook” is this workbook, complete — no Grade 2 student textbook is in hand (see the section introduction).

## Bulgaria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bulgaria's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 files = 4 works, all pupil textbooks: the Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika) *Математика* for Grades 1, 2 and 3 and for Grade 4 in two part-files (1 част / 2 част), 642 PDF pages in all** — and placed 123 teaching items. **The expansion map's “5 student + 0 teacher” is CORRECT as a file count (5 files, 4 works). Full-book vs sample verdict: NO in-hand Maths file is a sample** — every file's extent matches its own imprint's print-sheet count (Grade 1: 20.5 sheets, 128 lessons to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices; Grade 2: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 3: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 4: 10 + 11.5 sheets, lessons 1–136), and the 8 proven publisher samples in Bulgaria's library record (Music G6, Foreign Language G6/G7, БЕЛ G10/G11 and Geography G5) contain no Maths book, so the library's sample ruling does not touch this set. All five PDFs are image-only with no usable text layer and were read visually from page renders (cover, imprint, the book's own contents, and body pages for every distinct topic); printed folio = PDF page − 1 in every book, and Grade 4 Part 2 restarts its own pagination (its citations are marked “Part 2”). No Bulgaria Maths book was in the course before this merge — the string “Bulgaria” occurred nowhere under `maths/` — so every placed item is new here. Set boundary: everything else Bulgarian Maths in the records is exact-link / reader only and NOT in hand — Riva Maths G1–G4 PDFs (duplicate slots for the same grades), Klett epub Maths G5/G6 readers, Klett FlipHTML5 Maths G7/G8 (fullness unproven) and Архимед Maths G10/G11 Ourboox PDFs — none is part of this read and none is cited here; if any is later secured and read, Bulgaria reverts to PARTIAL until it is stocked. The placements' named exclusions — each grade's diagnostics, self-checks and consolidation lessons, drills, blitz tournaments, games and projects (other than the Grade 4 data project stocked in the statistics lessons), end-of-unit and year reviews, and the Grade 1 book's 12 cut-out appendices (number cards, shape cut-outs, play money, tangram, clock face, addition wheel, 0–20 ruler) — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; the topics they practise are counted at the lessons that teach them. **Book-internal flag, carried exactly: the Grade 1 book's introduction (printed p. 4) promises the unit litre, but no lesson teaches it — the contents list no litre lesson and the closing units summary (lesson 127, printed p. 155, read on the body page) lists only hour, euro, eurocent, kilogram and centimetre — so the litre is stocked nowhere, and Bulgaria Grade 1 stands as teaching cm, kg, euro/eurocent and the hour, not the litre.** Book errors proper (wrong mathematics) were found nowhere in the pages read; contents-page slips (e.g. Grade 4 Part 1 listing its angle lessons out of order in the contents) are apparatus slips, not teaching errors, and are counted nowhere. The placements' one NEW LESSON NEEDED topic — calculator use (Grade 4 Part 2, lessons 118–119, item 121) — is stocked in the existing `money-and-time/calculator-use` lesson built by the Sri Lanka expansion merge; no second calculator lesson is built. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items (items 52, 54, 55, 59, 64, 66, 86, 111, 118 (the multiplication side of the dual items)).

**Book 2:** *Математика за 2 клас* (pupil textbook, Grade 2) — Bulgaria, Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika), 2nd edition 2026, ISBN 978-619-252-077-9; seven authors headed by Таня Иванова Вълкова; 121,741,253 bytes, 146 PDF pp.; printed folio = PDF page − 1. FULL BOOK (112 lessons, contents to printed p. 140). Pupil textbook.

**Book 3:** *Математика за 3 клас* (pupil textbook, Grade 3) — Bulgaria, Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika), 1st edition 2018, ISBN 978-619-7457-41-4; seven authors headed by Таня Иванова Вълкова; 90,336,271 bytes, 146 PDF pp.; printed folio = PDF page − 1. FULL BOOK (112 lessons, contents to printed p. 140). Pupil textbook.

**Book 5:** *Математика за 4 клас, 2 част* (pupil textbook, Grade 4, part 2) — Bulgaria, Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika), 1st edition 2019, ISBN 978-619-7523-29-4; same authors as part 1; 20,143,337 bytes, 94 PDF pp. FULL BOOK — part 2 of 2 (lessons 63–136 in the part's own pagination, contents to printed p. 90); Part 2 restarts its own pagination (printed folio = PDF page − 1) and its citations are marked “Part 2”. Pupil textbook.

## Djibouti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Djibouti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 document, the CRIPEN *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher's guide, series « À MOI LES MATHS », édition 2023; 129 pages); 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 68 teaching items. The document is a teacher's guide: for every lesson of the matching pupil manuel it states the headline objective, the pedagogical intention, the pupil objectives, the lesson conduct, the activities and the worked answers. Every Djibouti item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guide teaches teachers how to teach the topic; it is never a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Djibouti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Djibouti pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the guide states (printed pp. 9–10) that since the 2019 school year teachers and pupils have a matching pupil manuel and livret of the same series (« À MOI LES MATHS », 5ème année), organised in the same four eight-week modules — those pupil books exist but are not in hand (placements record item R1), the guide's references to the manuel's pages are apparatus, not pupil content read here, and the student Maths files the library records locate are link-only partial extracts, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. Printed page = PDF page throughout the guide. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson sheet's headline objective and pupil objectives, splitting a sheet where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the pedagogical apparatus around each sheet (situation conduct, activity-by-activity commentary, exercise commentaries and synthesis questions) is not re-counted per activity. The placements' named exclusions — the avant-propos, préambule and présentation (printed pp. 3, 8–12: the competency approach, the four “moments of study”, the teacher's role, evaluation and remediation apparatus), the sommaire itself (printed pp. 4–7; its faults are placements record items R2 and R4), the mental-calculation routines (PLM) opening most sheets, per-sheet conduct and commentary counted once per content, and the guide's cross-references to the pupil manuel and livret pages — are stocked nowhere; the one content-bearing statement in the front matter, the end-of-primary objective's domain list (printed p. 9), is placed as a programme item in the ratio-and-proportion lesson. The Module 2 sheet at printed pp. 55–56 is a near-verbatim reprint of the Module 1 sheet at printed pp. 14–15 (placements record item R3); its content is placed once, as item 1. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide, Grade 5) — Djibouti, CRIPEN (Ministère de l'Éducation Nationale et de la Formation Professionnelle), édition 2023, series « À MOI LES MATHS »; 1,494,038 bytes, 129 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Calculating the product of two numbers with a one-digit multiplier, by the set-out technique and by decomposition | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 28–29 |
| The multiplication technique as consolidation: decomposing a number into tens and units and multiplying a number by a one-digit number | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 47–48 |

Djibouti enters this lesson as teacher-guide evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — the only document in Djibouti's in-hand set is a CRIPEN teacher's guide (Grade 5, édition 2023), not a pupil textbook, cited as the evidence kind it is, never counted as pupil teaching (the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent). Djibouti's class for Maths is PARTIAL (teacher-evidence-only): the 1 in-hand book is read in full and stocked, but no pupil textbook is in hand — the matching « À MOI LES MATHS » Grade 5 pupil manuel and livret exist and are named by the guide itself, but no complete pupil Maths file is held, and the located student Maths files are link-only partial extracts, unread.
