Every file was grabbed through the authenticated GitHub API route and verified byte-for-byte — each file's size equals its GitHub Contents metadata and its locally computed git blob SHA-1 equals the blob SHA recorded by GitHub; no mirror copy was used for any of the 21 files (the standing warning about librosdelmineduc.cl mirror editions is settled for this held set). The files filed as Grade 12 (the student book and the teacher guide) are byte-identical to the Grade 11 files — MINEDUC issues this level as ONE combined 3°–4° Medio volume filed under both grades — so each is cited once, at Grade 11, and the set's headline is 19 distinct books behind 21 filed rows wherever the count is used. All 11 distinct student books are complete full-year books (index, all units and end matter — glossary / answer key / bibliography — verified in place in every volume) and their items are COUNTED normally. **Every held teacher guide is a TOMO 2 partial volume** — covering student Units 3–4 (Grade 4: Units 3–5; the combined 3°–4° Medio guide: the 4° Medio year only, its 3° Medio guidance sitting in the unheld Tomo 1); **no Tomo 1 is held for any grade**, so the guide items below are teacher evidence only, restricted to exactly the units each Tomo 2 contains, named in the rows and never in the acceptance count. ## Finland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Finland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **24 files / 23 distinct works of government-hosted open material (OPH and the aoe.fi Library of Open Educational Resources); Finland has NO national textbook series** — and placed 173 items (51 pupil + 122 teacher evidence). The records' kind labels are wrong for most of the set: 13 of the 18 Mathematics-labelled “student” files are actually teacher-facing (in-service course books, theory texts, activity series and instructor guides), and only 6 files are pupil-facing (the Metsämatikkaa cards, the quadrilateral chart, the weather game, the Päivänselvää chapter, the programming workbook and its model solutions) — every book below is carried at its TRUE kind, never the record label. One byte-identical duplicate (aoe.2674-alku = aoe.2686-alku, the same file under two aoe records) is cited once; the Päivänselvää book is a Chapter-2-only excerpt, cited only for what it holds; the quadrilateral chart's filed copy is 1 content page + 175 blank padding pages. The placements' named exclusions — the weather game's non-temperature content, the computing-pedagogy matter in the two Turku course books, the motivation and mindset material in the JoMa books, the Pikkumatikoita parts 2, 8 and 11, and the programming books' pure-programming tasks — are stocked nowhere. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-evidence items are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 teacher-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3 and Book 4.

**Book 1:** *Näppituntuma 2.lk* (Grade 2 teacher book) — Finland, Opetushallitus (OPH) / Oulun Matikkamaa, Junttila & Ristola, 2012; 1,980,041 bytes, 65 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an OPH supplementary teacher activity collection, expressly “not tied to any textbook”; Finland has no national textbook series.

**Book 2:** *Kohti joustavaa matemaattista ajattelua* (teacher course book, JoMa project, aoe.2662) — Finland; 3,597,420 bytes, 54 PDF pp.; cited by PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an in-service course book on flexible mathematical thinking; the record labels it “student”. Its Swedish parallel edition (*Mot flexibelt matematiskt tänkande*, aoe.2662, 58 pp.) is the same work and is cited here, not separately.

**Book 3:** *Lumikuorma — Työpajan ohjaajan opas* (workshop instructor guide, aoe.4480) — Finland, LUMA Centre Lapland; 558,333 bytes, 3 PDF pp.; cited by PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the record says “student”, grade-05; the document is an instructor's guide addressing grades 5–7. Book error carried corrected: PDF p. 3 gives a small car's average mass as “40 g” — impossible for a car; taught corrected (a small car is of the order of 1,000 kg). Its worked snow example (1.77 kg ÷ 0.0177 m² = 100 kg/m²) is correct.

**Book 4:** *Ohjelmoinnillinen ajattelu ja ohjelmointiympäristöt* (teacher text, JoMa project, aoe.2692) — Finland; 641,706 bytes, 21 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 3. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an interpretation of curriculum goal T20 (algorithmic thinking) for lower-secondary teachers; the record labels it “student”.

## Dominican Republic added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Dominican Republic's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 books, all of them student books (0 teacher guides exist in hand)**: the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books of MINERD's Proyecto Libro Abierto, Serie 1 (*Matemática* 2°–6° Primaria and *Matemática* 1°–6° Secundaria), plus the mathematics content of the 3 integrated *Primero* volumes (Grade 1 has no dedicated *Matemática* book; only the three volumes' Matemática blocks are placed) — 3,184 PDF pages in all — and placed 661 teaching items (614 from the dedicated books, 47 from the Primero volumes). Every book was grabbed fresh by the authenticated GitHub API route, verified byte-exact against its staging `books.json` byte count and cover/title-proved, and the grabbed copy deleted after reading; the recorded Google Drive fallback is login-walled from this host and delivered no book. The expansion map's “16 student” count is wrong as a count of *Matemática* books: the dedicated set is 11, and 16 is reachable only by adding integrated all-subject files that are not *Matemática* books. Printed page = PDF page in every book (verified on the unit dividers and page footers). The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught topic — a named section or a coherent teaching block inside a unit. The placements' named exclusions — the apparatus of all 14 books (unit-opening, group-activity, evaluation, self-assessment, bibliography and book-care pages, except the five group activities that teach a topic found nowhere else in their book, which are placed); the *Primero Volumen I* Matemática diagnostic evaluation; the Primero competency pages; the Primero volumes' non-maths sequences (with two maths-adjacent blocks inside them named in the placements and stocked nowhere); the 5 Nivel Inicial integrated books (a scope note, not part of the Maths set); and one record artefact that is not a book (a stray partial-upload `.part` file in the grade-04 push state) — are stocked nowhere. One book error is carried corrected on the row it touches: *Matemática 3° Primaria*'s weight table (evaluation, printed p. 152; the same table family at p. 147) pairs 8 lb with 48 oz — wrong: 1 lb = 16 oz, so 8 lb = 128 oz (48 oz is 3 lb). Minor slips, no action: the contents lists of *Matemática* 2° and 3° Secundaria run one page behind the printed folios — every citation uses the folios. Checked and NOT an error: *Matemática 4° Secundaria*, printed p. 84, prints Pythagoras as a² = b² + c² with a defined as the hypotenuse — correct as printed. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–14 in the placements' order. The Dominican Republic COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these student-book rows. The Dominican Republic's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books plus the Primero volumes carrying Grade 1's maths) has been read in full and placed.

Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4 and Book 14.

## Guyana added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Guyana's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 book, a student (pupil) textbook: *Let's Do Mathematics Book 5* (Ministry of Education, Georgetown, Guyana — Easy Path Series), the Grade 5 book; 0 teacher guides** — and placed 140 teaching items. The expansion map's count of 1 student + 0 teacher is CORRECT, proved from five records (textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json, phase3-push-state-lane2.json, the staging books.json files and download-lists/guyana.md): Guyana has 6 books in hand in total and exactly one is Mathematics. The other five in-hand books (G1 *Science Around Us Book 1*, G1 *Social Studies For Our Children Book 1*, G2 *Fun With Language Book 2 Part 1*, G3 *Fun With Language Book 3*, G6 *Science Around Us Book 6*) were each byte-verified against their records and their covers/title pages read — each is the book its record names; **no maths book is hiding under another subject label**. All other Guyana Maths books in the records (Easy Path Books 1–4 and 6, the nursery numeracy workbooks and the “Mathematics Made Easy” series) are link-only — exact URLs recorded, no file in hand — and were not read. The one book was read from staging in place: 5,990,840 bytes (= the books.json record), 169 PDF pages, MD5 c6128b33f8842397e0351776a7f36929, and it is the COMPLETE book, not a partial excerpt — the contents page (PDF p. 7) lists all 30 units, every unit start was verified at its contents page, and the text runs printed pp. 1–158, ending with the final “Let Us Look Back” exercises (printed p. 158) and the back cover. Printed page = PDF page − 8 throughout (Unit 1 begins printed p. 1 = PDF p. 9). Book observation F1, carried exactly: the cover reads “Revised Edition 2004” (the staging books.json and the checklist follow the cover) while the imprint page (PDF p. 2) reads “© 2005 … Revised 2005” — one consistent scan of one book, so the edition is cited as “Revised Edition 2004 (cover); imprint © 2005”. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught skill or concept, at the point the book teaches it. The placements' named exclusions — front matter (acknowledgements, foreword, preface, contents; PDF pp. 1–8); every end-of-unit REVIEW section and every “LET US LOOK BACK” evaluative set (after Units 5, 10, 15, 20, 25 and at the end of Unit 30, printed pp. 156–158 — revision and assessment exercises that restate items already placed, no new skill taught); worked story problems and exercise sets inside the units (practice of the item taught on that page, not separate items); and the example exchange rates themselves (dated examples that differ between Units 20 and 21 by design; the taught skill stocked is the conversion method) — are stocked nowhere. Guyana COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these pupil-book rows. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items from Book 1 (items 23, 25, 27, 28, 29).

## Gabon added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Gabon's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **17 documents: 6 pupil textbooks (the CIAM *Mathématiques* volumes for 6e, 5e, 4e, 2e Scientifique, 1re SM and 1re Littéraire, EDICEF), 6 teacher guides (the *Super Efficace* guides pédagogiques for 1re–5e année, EDIG/EDICEF, and the CIAM *Mathématiques 2e S* guide pédagogique) and 5 curriculum documents (the IPN *Curriculum Mathématiques* for 1re–5e année)** — every file grabbed fresh for the read, its byte size and page count matching the records exactly and its identity proved from cover/title/sommaire pages — and placed 522 items: 83 pupil-textbook teaching items, 389 teacher-evidence items and 50 curriculum-requirement items. The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. There are no partial volumes and no duplicates in the set: every CIAM scan runs from its first chapter to its own index at the sommaire's stated page (the 6e scan opens at printed p. 3 — the cover and title leaves are not in the scan, but all teaching content is present, printed pp. 3–222 = the PDF's 220 pages exactly). The two misleading historic Drive filenames are settled by the books' own title pages: the file named “2nde SM” is the CIAM 2e Scientifique pupil book, and the file named “2nde C” is its 2e S Guide pédagogique. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. Counting grain: one curriculum item per palier × compétence statement; one guide item per lesson the guide teaches; one pupil item per sommaire chapter. Book errors are carried corrected where they land: the Curriculum 2e année, Compétence 2, Palier 1 prints “le passionnément des objets l'espace”, a typo for “positionnement” (positioning of objects in space), and is stocked corrected; the Curriculum 3e année, Compétence 1, Palier 2 states “nombres de 1001 à 1999 (0 à 1999)” — the parenthetical contradicts the main range, and the statement is stocked by its main range; the CIAM 4e index spells PPMC as “PPCM” (trivial, noted for the record). The placements' named exclusions — the guides' remediation/application apparatus between paliers; the curricula's front matter and framework apparatus (rationale, timetables, competency-framework and assessment sections); the CIAM pupil books' end-of-chapter exercise sets, préfaces, authors' letters and closing indexes; the 2e S pupil book's Greek-alphabet page; the 2e S guide's synoptic programme tables — are stocked nowhere. Gabon stocks only evidence rows in this lesson — teacher-evidence and curriculum-requirement, with no pupil-textbook row — so it is named in the countries line and in the rows but is NOT added to this lesson's acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 22 items (19 teacher-evidence, 3 curriculum-requirement) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 5, Book 6, Book 7, Book 8.

## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 12 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 2*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 3 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 4 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 5 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*).

**Book 1:** *Mathematics Year 2* — the book calls itself the Year 2 Mathematics Activity book — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 233,595,104 bytes, 70 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Printed page = PDF page − 2. Scan defect: printed p. 57 appears twice (PDF pp. 59–60 are the same leaf) and printed p. 58 is absent — the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand and is cited nowhere.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics Year 3* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 37,315,079 bytes, 80 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Printed page = PDF page − 3 for the in-sequence body; the scan order is scrambled at the end and the capacity-discovery activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51), cited once.

**Book 3:** *Mathematics Year 4* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 96,814,549 bytes, 86 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Folios cited as printed in the book; the folio sequence is irregular (folios 44–48 and 54 never appear, and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice) — the leaf count closes exactly and every contents strand and unit is present, so the gaps are the book's own folio misprints, not missing leaves.

**Book 4:** *Year 5 Mathematics* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit; 2,935,466 bytes, 98 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. The held capture has no cover/title leaves: it opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, printed pp. 1–98; the book's identity rests on the staged record and the body's own structure.

**Book 5:** *Year 6 Mathematics Textbook* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit; 3,706,247 bytes, 91 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. The interior copyright/acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy error in the book itself; cover, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout.

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items — 7 pupil-textbook rows and 2 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado*), Book 2 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 4to grado*), Book 8 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 2do grado*), Book 9 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas and 4to grado*).

The expansion map is WRONG in substance for Mauritius (flag F2): it recorded “1 student / 0 teacher” in hand, but the counted file is a supplementary worksheets excerpt, not a pupil textbook, and the Progress Book was not counted at all; the true in-hand Maths-content set is 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 1 pupil worksheets excerpt and 1 progress-record book. Flag F1: the GitHub record marks the worksheets `quarters: "full"` and the file is named `pupil-full-year.pdf`, but the file itself is 7 PDF pages carrying printed pp. 1–5 only — whatever else Part 1 contains is not in hand, and the excerpt is cited only for the pages actually held (both flags are record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records). All other Mauritius Maths books — the MIE primary and secondary Mathematics textbooks (exact links only) and Prevocational Mathematics Year 2 (ministry link only, extra-grid) — are link-only, not in hand and not read; the other in-hand Mauritius files (Road Safety Education Grades 1–6, *A Pictorial Primer — Say No to Tobacco*, Kreol Morisien textbooks, the Pre-Primary curriculum documents) are other subjects, verified by a full census of the Mauritius folders. The placements' named exclusions — Book A's front matter (cover, Numeracy Panel page, copyright); the Grade 7 colour-naming competency (no mathematics content as stated; the pattern competency it prepares for is counted at Grade 8); the Grade 9 “perform arithmetic operations mentally” process statement; the incomplete printed fragment “including algebraic fractions ( )” at Grade 9+; the Progress Book's non-Mathematics subject sections and its blank record apparatus; and record items R1–R3 (a Physical Education cross-reference, the front-matter GPA worked example, and the worksheets cover's external game link) — are stocked nowhere. The Progress Book's book errors are carried corrected on the rows they touch: the Grade 7 fraction list prints ½ twice (item 17); the H.C.F./L.C.M. word-problem requirement is stated a second time, misplaced inside §1.6 Decimals (item 94); three statements are truncated in the book — “involving brackets of the form”, the form never given (item 145), “a^n for n = −1, −2, −3 and rational” (item 153), and “or in the form” (item 216); §5.2 is headed “Pictograms and bar charts” but states only frequency-table work (item 151); π is printed as the Cyrillic letter “л” (item 172); and the quadratic rows are printed under the §3.5 Matrices heading with the general form garbled as “( a 2+b +c)”, read as ax² + bx + c (items 199–202). Mauritius's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: only the 10 taught items rest on pupil material, and that from a 5-page excerpt, so Mauritius is named in the countries line and in the rows below but is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Bahamas/Canada evidence precedent. ## Nauru added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nauru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **10 documents, all of them Learning Through Doing (LTD) *Mathematics Planning Documents*, Nauru editions (2026) — Prep and Years 1–8, plus the LTD *Scope and Sequence, Years 1–8* — teacher planning alignments that sequence an Australian commercial subscription lesson system against the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus, every lesson keyed to a Nauru curriculum code (NRU-*); 0 pupil (student) textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 0 workbooks** — and placed 238 items, every one of them PLANNING/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE: the Nauru syllabus's own key-idea statements as printed on each document's closing “Nauru Curriculum Alignment” pages. Nauru publishes no government textbooks (country record, download list §§F–Q, seven fronts), so Nauru's standing in the course is the syllabus-evidence reckoning: Nauru is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count is right in number and wrong in kind: it carries Nauru as “10 student Maths books”, but every one of the 10 books.json entries is kind `planning-document` and states in its own approval note that it is NOT a Nauru government student textbook (diff fault F1 — a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge). textbooks.tsv carries 12 Nauru rows, all of them USP IOE ESD items, and zero Mathematics rows — the 10 planning documents live only in books.json and push-state. Australia overlap is ZERO: the Australia read's nine Cambridge/Jacaranda Years 8–10 pupil textbooks share no title, ISBN, byte size or file with this set, and the QCE senior titles cross-referenced in Nauru's country record are link-only, in hand for neither country and read by neither reader; nothing from the Australia placements is cited here. All 10 files were read from local disk in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records (334,327–887,725 bytes; 67 PDF pages in total; 10 distinct MD5s, no duplicates inside the set); nothing was downloaded. Printed-page basis: the documents carry no printed folios (the Scope's footer “Page N” equals its PDF page), so pages cited are document (PDF) pages — Prep key ideas on alignment pp. 6–7 of 7, Years 1–8 on alignment p. 6 of 6; the term overviews (p. 1) and weekly plans (pp. 2–5) sequence the same key ideas and add none of their own. Counting grain: one item per NRU key idea. The *Scope and Sequence* (887,725 bytes, 13 PDF pages) was read in full and cross-checked — every Scope topic traces to a year document's lessons under the codes placed — so it contributes 0 items of its own; counting it again would double-count one programme as two sources. Book errors found on the read are placed corrected, as the placements record them (diff fault F2): Year 3 NRU-M3.3 prints “suing” for “using” and NRU-G3.3 prints “jey” for “key”; Year 5 NRU-CD5.4 prints “recoding sheets” for “recording sheets”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.4 prints “co-ordiinates”; the Year 8 alignment page prints its first geometry code as “NRU-G9.1” — a Year 9 code inside the Year 8 document — while the Year 8 weekly plans cite the same lessons as NRU-G8.1, and item 226 is placed as the Year 8 item it is; the Year 2 document's Term 4 plan cites “NRU-N3.4” against Year 2 strategy-review lessons, where the alignment page's NRU-N2.4 governs; several weekly-plan code strings are garbled in print — the alignment pages' code lists are clean and govern throughout. The placements also record a document inconsistency (diff fault F3): the Scope's fractions table opens its halves entry at the earliest (Prep) level, but the Prep planning document contains no fractions anywhere — fractions enter the year documents at Year 1 (NRU-N1.8) — and the placements follow the year documents. Some key ideas are explicitly Nauru-localised (Year 3 NRU-G3.3: simple maps of Nauru; Year 6 NRU-CD6.4: data contexts “familiar to Nauruan students”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.6: graphs “using data from Nauru”). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus aims paragraph printed in every year document (general aims, no subject content); the term overviews and weekly plans as separate items (they sequence the same key ideas, and the LTD lesson titles are the Australian system's commercial catalogue, delivered by subscription — the planning documents do not reproduce the lessons, so no lesson content is placed, only the syllabus key ideas the lessons are sequenced against); “Assessment Opportunities” headers and blank assessment columns (apparatus); the Scope & Sequence's duplicated topic statements across years (one programme, counted once at key-idea grain); the 12 USP IOE ESD files filed under Nauru (not Mathematics, excluded by subject); and the OER4Pacific COL open-schooling Mathematics 11/12 PDFs named in the download list §G (regional OER, never staged under Nauru, not in hand, not read). Into this lesson the placements stock 10 planning/curriculum-evidence items from Book 1 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 1, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 2 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 2, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 3 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 3, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 4 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 4, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 5 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 5, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 6 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 6, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 7 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 7, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 8 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 8, Nauru edition (2026)*).

## Peru added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Peru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 pupil WORKBOOKS of the Ministerio de Educación (MINEDU): *Cuaderno de trabajo Matemática* for Grades 4, 1 and 2** — and placed 238 items, one per *ficha* (the workbook's own numbered teaching unit). The expansion map's count is right and its kind label is wrong (record correction C1): the 3 are **workbooks**, not student textbooks — no Peru pupil *texto*, teacher guide or secondary Maths book is in hand, and every other Peru Maths title in the records (Primaria Matemática 3, 5, 6; Secundaria *Resolvamos problemas* 1–5; *Fichas de Matemática* 1–5; the multigrado/rural *textos*) is link-only, never downloaded. The fourth secured Peru file, the Grade 1 *Ciencia y Tecnología* fichas booklet (7 PDF pages, 18,486,216 bytes), was checked first-hand and is science only — no maths content — and is cited nowhere here. A workbook is a pupil book: every item below is **teaching content** (each ficha teaches its topic through worked situations, definitions in context and guided practice), so Peru's rows are pupil rows, COUNTED in full — the workbook set closes as a teaching set, not an evidence-only one (the South Africa pattern). The secured files are **complete volumes** (each runs from its cover to the standard MINEDU civics back matter at its printed end) and are not duplicates of one another (distinct byte sizes and MD5s). They are, though, **later printings than the records describe** (record correction C2): all three are the 2021 Dotación-2022 printings — Grade 4 the 5th edition of June 2021 (Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08052), not the 2016 first edition of its download-list mirror note; Grade 1 likewise the 5th edition of June 2021 (Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08267), not the 3rd edition of 2019 (Dotación 2020) its mirror note describes — and Grade 2's credits page carries both the 2019 printing (C. P. N.° 002-2019-MINEDU/VMGP/UE 120, Dotación 2020) and the 5th edition of June 2021 (Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08050) over one shared reedition body; the citations below carry the printings actually read. Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page − 2 in all three books (footer-verified). Counting reach of the set: Grade 1 counts to 50, Grade 2 works with quantities to 99, Grade 4 represents quantities to the thousands (4-digit numbers in words and figures). The placements' record items are apparatus, stocked nowhere: the Grade 4 fraction-domino game (printed p. 157) and its *Recortables* cut-outs (printed pp. 147–166), the Grade 1 cut-out and sticker apparatus (printed pp. 189–279, incl. the 50-board and the “Ludo: Camino al 50” board game), the Grade 2 tangram answer key (*Solucionario del tangram*, printed p. 34) and its *Recortables* (printed pp. 221–238). Named exclusions, stocked nowhere: the civics back matter bound into all three printings (Carta Democrática Interamericana and Acuerdo Nacional excerpts, national symbols), the presentation and contents pages of each book, and the Grade 1 science booklet (out of subject, verified). Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items — all pupil-workbook rows — from Book 1 (*Cuaderno de trabajo Matemática 4*), Book 2 (*Cuaderno de trabajo Matemática 1*) and Book 3 (*Cuaderno de trabajo Matemática 2*).

N.° 007-2021-MINEDU/VMGP/UE 120; Depósito Legal BNP N.° 2021-08052); 77,728,421 bytes, 172 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 2. PUPIL WORKBOOK — counted: the workbook teaches each topic through worked situations, definitions in context and guided practice.

PUPIL WORKBOOK — counted: the workbook teaches each topic through worked situations, definitions in context and guided practice. ## Jamaica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Jamaica's whole in-hand Maths set from the files — **8 documents: 7 National Standards Curriculum (NSC) guides that carry a Mathematics section (Grades 1, 2 and 3 Integrated Studies / Language Arts / Mathematics; Grades 4, 5 and 6 Exploratory Core; and the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide — all Ministry of Education, Youth and Information, © 2018, the Grade 6 guide in its FINAL edition of Jan 08 2020), plus 1 pupil reader** — and placed 311 items: 308 curriculum-evidence items from the guides and 3 pupil-text items from the reader. **Kind split: 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 7 curriculum guides.** A curriculum guide states what is REQUIRED to be taught and how to teach it; it is not a pupil textbook, so every guide item below is CURRICULUM EVIDENCE, worded “curriculum requires”, and Jamaica is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The expansion map is wrong on both count and kind (flag F1, record-side, reported not fixed): it listed Jamaica as FILES-IN-HAND with 4 student / 0 teacher Maths books — its build matched the subject field only, counting the three Grades 1–3 guides and the Grades 7–9 guide and missing the three Grades 4–6 Exploratory Core guides, whose subject field reads only “Exploratory Core” while their title pages name Mathematics as one of the four core subjects; and the four files it did count are curriculum guides, not student books. Two further record faults ride with the set (record-side; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/jamaica.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv has **no row at all for the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide** (flag F2), although the file is in staging, in books.json and in the pushed set; and the TSV's Grade 5 row points at a Telegram link instead of the file's secured GitHub asset (flag F3) — the book was read byte-exact from that asset. One file defect, stated plainly: the Grade 5 guide's body font has a broken ToUnicode map, so its text layer extracts as bullet glyphs; its Mathematics section was read in full from rendered page images (120-DPI renders of PDF pp. 139–255 = printed pp. 122–238), every page legible, and it is counted as read on that basis. Printed-page basis: each guide prints its own folio; the Mathematics windows run printed = PDF − 5 (Grade 1), − 33 (Grade 2), − 16 (Grade 3), − 11 (Grade 4), − 17 (Grade 5) and − 12 (Grade 6), and Book 7's pages are its own per-grade section pages. Counting grain: Grades 1–3, one item per distinct numbered objective in the Mathematics Content Standards, plus NEW/Extended objectives and Scope-and-Sequence topics stated in no standard (marked (S&S)); Grades 4–6 and 7–9, one item per distinct objective in each Mathematics unit's objectives list; an objective restated verbatim is counted once, at its first full statement. Boundary files examined and excluded — stocked nowhere: the nursery *Birth to Three is Key* early-childhood guide (ages 0–3, all-domains; number concepts are one activity focus, with no Mathematics section or objectives at NSC grain — not a Maths book); the Grades 4–6 Resource & Technology guide (its file is a bundle whose opening leaves are the Grades 7–9 English guide; no Mathematics section); and the Civics (×2), English Language, Integrated Science and Social Studies guides (no Mathematics subject in any of them; the Science guide's measurement/graphing is science content). The placements' named exclusions inside the books are likewise stocked nowhere: the Grade 1 window's “Maths Prayers” (printed pp. 242–243 — devotional poems, religious apparatus, no mathematics content); the Scope and Sequence charts, Process Strand checklists, Illustrative Activities, Benchmarks and Illustrative Examples as apparatus (topics stated only there are placed as items and marked (S&S)); the Integrated Studies and Language Arts windows of the Grades 1–3 guides, which restate the same Mathematics attainment targets as delivery vehicles and state no requirement beyond the window's standards; the Grade 1 book's Mathematics lesson plans and “Teaching Mathematics: Ideas & Activities” (printed pp. 340–400, teacher apparatus); Book 7's Appendices A–E and closing sample lesson plan (printed pp. 191–206, activity apparatus and worked examples of the same objectives); and the reader's copyright/credits leaves. Book errors and faults are carried as the placements record them, taught corrected where they touch a row: the Grade 1 fractions standard (printed p. 275) cross-refers an objective as “Objective 2.3.27” — a Grade 2 bank number inside the Grade 1 book — and objective numbers with a leading “2.” recur throughout the Grade 1 standards (the bank numbering is shared across grades; each such objective is placed once, as stated for Grade 1); the Grade 2 addition example on printed p. 268 (“Pat has 43 oranges … How many apples Pat has now?”) mixes fruits mid-problem — a wording slip, the objective it illustrates placed as read; two Grade 2 standards pages carry a mojibake line (a Caesar-shifted “See previous page for Attainment Targets and Objectives”) — a print-production artefact, no content lost; the Grade 3 window and its Scope and Sequence state **no decimals** anywhere, so no Grade 3 decimals row is stocked; the Grade 4 Term 3 Unit 2 outcome names “cross-sections” of cubes/cuboids, which the unit nowhere teaches — placed inside its item as named-only; the Grade 4 probability unit's header prints “GRAGE 4” (sic); the Grade 5 Overview disagrees with its own units three times, recorded as printed (division of five-digit numbers sits in Term 3 Unit 1, not Term 2; the Overview's Volume has no objective anywhere in the Grade 5 Mathematics section and is stocked nowhere; lines of symmetry sit in Term 2 Unit 3, while the Term 3 Geometry unit is circles, polygons to eight sides, and pyramids); the Grade 6 Term 3 Unit 1 tyre-pressure activity is Science-linked and its arithmetic is already inside its unit's item, not placed separately; in Book 7, the Grade 9 ratio unit's footer prints “NSC Mathematics: Grade 7” (a template error — the unit sits in the Grade 9 section and is placed as Grade 9), the matrices unit header prints “GRADE 9 TERM 1: NUMBERS” although the unit sits in the Term 3 sequence (placed as Term 3), the Grade 7 constructions unit's objectives page repeats the transformations unit's first objective (counted once, in the transformations item), and the Grade 8 Term 2 geometry unit carries a header title copied from the algebra unit while its objectives are polygons and angle sums (placed by its objectives). Jamaica's pupil Maths textbooks are commercial (Carlong / Hodder / Macmillan etc.) — the library holds none; they stand in the download list only, not in hand and not read. Into this lesson the placements stock 10 curriculum-evidence rows from Book 2, Book 3, Book 4 and Book 5.

Mathematics window printed pp. 234–296; printed p. = PDF p. − 33. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the same standing as Book 1.

**Book 3:** *Grade 3 NSC Guide: Integrated Studies | Language Arts | Mathematics* (curriculum guide) — Jamaica, Ministry of Education, Youth and Information (MoEYI), © 2018; 22,514,507 bytes, 474 PDF pp., MD5 1be3a4158691656aa6d81c9635ef2846. Mathematics window printed pp. 299–379; printed p. = PDF p. − 16. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY. No decimals are stated anywhere in this window or its Scope and Sequence, and no Grade 3 decimals row is stocked from it.

Jamaica enters this lesson as curriculum evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — Jamaica's in-hand Maths set holds no pupil textbook and no teacher guide, only the seven National Standards Curriculum guides, which state what must be taught without being pupil teaching material themselves (the standing evidence-only precedent). Jamaica's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): every in-hand document has been read in full and stocked, and it advances only when a Jamaica pupil Maths textbook lands in hand and is read.

## Haiti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Haiti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **28 files, every one of them teacher-side**: the nine *Programme à Compétences Minimales* (PCM) volumes for Années Fondamentales 1–9 (MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental, November 2019), the three IPN *Programme détaillé, Matières de base* volumes (4e, 5e, 6e année), the three *Programme Pédagogique Opérationnel* (PPO) Mathématiques fascicules for the 3e cycle (7e, 8e, 9e année, 1989-1990), the eight Nectar (MENFP) teacher sequences (four for 7e année, four for 8e année), the Nouveau Secondaire *Programme-Cadre* and the four NS detailed programmes (NS1–NS4) — every one of the 28 carrying a full Mathématiques section — and placed **1,639 items, every one of them TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE**: **Haiti has no pupil Maths textbook in hand under any label**, so Haiti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Ghana / Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count for Haiti is wrong: it carries 15, counting only the books.json entries whose subject field reads “Mathematics”, and the download list claims 43 teacher files; the true maths-bearing set is 28 files — the nine “All subjects” PCM volumes, the three IPN detailed programmes and the NS Programme-Cadre all carry full maths sections the count missed (a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge); textbooks.tsv is likewise a stale 28-row index naming only a subset of the secured files. Haiti's official pupil books (the MENFP/FAGEM *Manuels uniques* / “Mon manuel” series) are in hand for other subjects only, never for Mathematics, and the two remanié programme leads (a 7e année fascicule and a 4e année detailed programme) are not in hand — and programme documents are not textbooks under the 2026-11-10 textbook-test ruling in any case — so **Haiti stays PARTIAL for Maths, teacher evidence only**. All 28 files were read page-by-page from the secured PDFs in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records; nothing was downloaded. Read depth: the PPO 8e fascicule and the IPN 4e and 6e programmes are image-only scans and were read by OCR in full, spot-checked against the page images — where OCR garbled a worked example's digits, the placement teaches the corrected arithmetic and the case is flagged; PPO 7e PDF p. 48 and PPO 9e PDF pp. 20, 30, 31 and 32 were verified blank, separator or title pages carrying no objectives. Printed-page bases: PCM printed page = PDF page − 1; IPN 4e and 6e printed page = PDF page − 9, IPN 5e printed folios equal PDF pages; PPO 7e printed = PDF − 3, PPO 8e printed folio = PDF page, PPO 9e printed = PDF − 2; Programme-Cadre NS printed = PDF − 9 (verified against the detailed programme at §5.1–§5.2); NS1 and NS2 printed = PDF page, NS3 cites PDF pages, NS4 printed = PDF − 1; the Nectar sequences cite PDF pages by séance. The placements' eleven flags are all document-side and are carried here exactly: the PCM 6e volume's maths section header mislabels its level “5ème AF” (F1 — its items are cited to the 6e volume they stand in); PCM 5e's spoken-number row slips (“six mille deux” for 6,002, and a thousands-family slip in item 352's row) are placed corrected (F2); the PCM “orthocentre” definition error stands in the PCM **7e** volume — the diff's “(AF8)” label is the diff's own slip, disproven against the live PCM 8e section, which carries the correct circumcentre definition (F3); PCM 9e's right-asymptote and strict-inequality rows are completions of points the volume leaves silent, not errors (F4); PCM 9e's opening “années diagnostiques” ((6e), 7e, 8e) reinstate the diagnostic year the 3e-cycle fascicules dropped (F5); the PCM 7e volume announces a simple-interest objective that does not exist anywhere in its grids — nothing is placed for it, and simple interest is taught by PCM 9e and PPO 9e (F6); the Nectar “Relations entre les angles” sequence states a false corresponding-angles criterion and a mise en train with no solution, and the “Calcul littéral” sequence's séance 5 example drops a factor of x — all placed as corrected (F7, F8); the record-side faults (the map's count, the download list's count, the stale textbooks.tsv) are reported, not fixed (F9); the IPN 6e worked examples carry numerical inconsistencies (objectives 5.1, 7.1, 12.2, 16.1, 19.1, 21.1, 22.1) flagged as read and NOT asserted as print errors — the volume is an OCR-read scan and some may be digit misreads (F10); and NS2's own dating (2015 in the detailed programmes' shared headers, the school year 2008-2009 on its cover) is the book's own, not a discrepancy (F11). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the PCM volumes' other-discipline sections and their week-by-week scheduling columns beyond each distinct objective; the IPN volumes' maths avant-propos, theme indexes, other-subject sections and Suggestions/Évaluation apparatus (except where an activity is itself the taught content); the PPO fascicules' shared front matter, Programme-Cadre theme tables, progression grids, bibliographies and annexes; the Nectar sequences' summative-evaluation séances and session-management apparatus (except where a séance's activity is the taught content); the Programme-Cadre's other subjects, and the Nouveau Secondaire framework chapters shared between it and the NS1/NS2 volumes (placed once, from the Programme-Cadre volume); and the verified blank and separator pages. Into this lesson the placements stock 26 items — all teacher/curriculum-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*PCM, 1ère AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 2 (*PCM, 2ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 3 (*PCM, 3ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 4 (*PCM, 4e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 5 (*PCM, 5e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 6 (*PCM, 6e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 7 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 4e année*) and Book 8 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 5e année*).

## Suriname added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Suriname's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **18 books: 13 pupil Maths books, 3 teacher Maths books and 2 kindergarten curriculum books** (20 files examined; two further in-hand files, the LJ3 OJW *Leerlingenwerkboek* and *Ik-boek*, were checked and excluded — drawing and orientation activities with no rekenen strand and no number teaching) — and placed **376 items: 344 pupil teaching items, 7 teacher-evidence items and 25 curriculum-evidence items**. No Suriname row existed in any maths lesson before this merge, so every placed item is new here. **Set verdict, proved from the books themselves:** the expansion map's count is right for the Mathematics-labelled set (13 pupil + 3 teacher = 16 files), but its kind labels are wrong in three places: (1) the LJ6 “Leerkrachtenhandleiding Deel 1” of the records is the programme's **Algemene Inleiding** volume (Book 14), not a lesson-by-lesson guide to Deel 1; (2) the two LJ7 teacher books are **oplossingenboeken — answer keys** (Books 15–16), teacher evidence; (3) Book 4 is a **rekenboek** (lesson book), not the “Leerlingenboek” textbooks.tsv names it — the same book. **Maths was hiding under another subject label once:** the KG *Spelend leren* Themaboek 3 and 4 (Books 17–18), filed as “Play-based learning” with textbooks.tsv kind “student” — also wrong: they are **teacher curriculum books** — each carry twelve *voorbereidend rekenen* (preparatory maths) sections with stated learning goals; their 24 items are stocked as **curriculum evidence**, never pupil teaching. **Partial volumes, named as such:** Books 4 and 5 are **Deel A only** and Book 12 is **Deel 1 only**; the download list's remark that LJ8 Deel 2 is “already held” is **not borne out by any record in hand** (textbooks.tsv, the staging books.json and the push-state carry only Deel 1 for LJ8) — LJ8 Deel 2 is treated as not in hand, and the LJ4/LJ5 B-volumes and LJ8 Deel 2–3 are link-only or unrecorded, cited nowhere. Book 8 (LJ6 Deel 3) is staged and pushed but has no textbooks.tsv row — the map counted it, the TSV does not carry it. **The six flagged claims are carried exactly:** (F1) **Book 11 (LJ7 Deel 3) has no Les 24** — contents and body both jump from Les 23 (tangram) to Les 25 (tekenen op schaal); the book holds 38 lessons, not 39, and the rows cite the 38 that exist; (F2) the LJ8-Deel-2 “already held” claim, above — contradicted, not in hand; (F3) **book error, Book 6 Les 6 (printed p. 23):** the information block adds a quarter hour to 15.45 and gives the result as “06:00” — the correct result is **16:00**, and the row teaches the corrected form; (F4) **book error, Book 7 Les 14 (printed p. 54):** the information states the area of a rectangle with a garbled formula line (the perimeter formula 2l + 2b is given correctly, and the area is worked by counting in the same lesson) — the row teaches perimeter = 2l + 2b and area by counting/calculation, the forms the lesson's own worked examples use; (F5) **book error, Book 6 Les 30 (printed p. 93):** the split-subtraction check line prints minus signs where the method needs the parts added (7,986 − 5,234 split as 2,000 + 700 + 50 + 2 = 2,752) — the method itself is taught correctly in Les 21 and the rows follow the correct form; (F6) **Book 13's kind:** textbooks.tsv files the LJ10 Reader as a pupil book; its foreword states it is a guide “voor de reken-leerkracht en leerling” (for teacher and pupil) — its items are placed as teaching content, worded as a **programme reader**, not a pupil textbook. **Text-layer caveat (not a claim):** Book 3's text layer drops the digit “1” in several headings (“splitsingen van 0” for the splitting of 10); the rows use the true values, read from the sums and context. **Checked and NOT flagged:** the answer keys (Books 15–16) match their pupil books lesson for lesson (39 and 38 lessons respectively), and Book 14's contents listing of the kwartaal-1 lessons matches Book 6. Byte sizes checkable against the gov.sr records matched exactly (Books 7, 8, 10, 11). Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page in every book except Books 17–18 (printed = PDF − 5). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the *Vooruit* books' week-closing revision pages (*weekafsluitingen*); Books 1–3's *herhaling* revision worksheets, *oefentoets* practice tests, *knipbladen* cut-out sheets and automatisation games, except where a sheet first teaches a point (those are placed); the *Plezier* books' closing games; Book 13's ch. 1 programme tables beyond item R1; and Books 17–18's non-maths theme sections. The *Plezier in rekenen* companion volumes found by the 2026-11-10 gov.sr census (LJ4 B leerlingenboek, LJ4 werkboek/takenboek, LJ5 takenboek/bijwerkboek) are link-only finds, not in hand — not read, not counted. Suriname counts in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil rows; its teacher rows and curriculum rows are evidence — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent). Into this lesson the placements stock 20 items (20 pupil) from Book 3 (*Plezier in rekenen, Leerlingenwerkboek 3 (LJ3)*), Book 4 (*Plezier in rekenen, rekenboek A (LJ4)*), Book 5 (*Plezier in rekenen, lessenboek 5a (LJ5)*), Book 6 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ6)*), Book 7 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 2 (LJ6)*), Book 8 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 3 (LJ6)*), Book 9 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ7)*), Book 10 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 2 (LJ7)*), Book 12 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ8)*) and Book 13 (*Reader Rekenen/Economie (LJ10)*).

**Book 3:** *Plezier in rekenen — Leerlingenwerkboek 3, Leerjaar 3* (pupil workbook) — Suriname, MinOV, 2018; 4,766,969 bytes, 60 PDF pp. Pupil workbook. Printed page = PDF page. (Text-layer caveat: this file's text layer drops the digit “1” in several headings — “splitsingen van 0” is the splitting of 10; the rows cite the true values, read from the sums and context.)

**Book 4:** *Plezier in rekenen — rekenboek A, Leerjaar 4* (pupil book) — Suriname, Minov, 2013 (the Surinamese version of *De Wereld in Getallen*); 17,741,213 bytes, 69 PDF pp. Pupil book. Printed page = PDF page. **Deel A only** — no Deel B in hand. (textbooks.tsv names it “Leerlingenboek”; it is a lesson book (rekenboek) — the same book.)

**Book 5:** *Plezier in rekenen — lessenboek 5a, Leerjaar 5* (pupil book) — Suriname, Minov, 2014; 10,033,668 bytes, 67 PDF pp. Pupil book. Printed page = PDF page. **Deel A only** — no Deel B in hand.

**Book 6:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 1, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, Ministerie van Onderwijs, Wetenschap en Cultuur (MinOWC), 2023 (made with VUB); 4,251,212 bytes, 96 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page. Kwartaal 1, lessons 1–30.

**Book 7:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 2, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2023; 5,713,419 bytes, 144 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page (byte size matches the gov.sr record exactly). Kwartaal 2, lessons 1–39.

**Book 8:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 3, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2023; 2,709,444 bytes, 136 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page (byte size matches the gov.sr record exactly). Kwartaal 3, lessons 1–39. (The book is staged and pushed but carries no textbooks.tsv row — a record gap, reported, not fixed by this merge.)

**Book 9:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 1, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 7* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2022; 10,291,128 bytes, 96 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page. Kwartaal 1, lessons 1–30.

**Book 10:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 2, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 7* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2022; 20,689,632 bytes, 120 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page (byte size matches the gov.sr record exactly). Kwartaal 2, lessons 1–39.

**Book 12:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 1, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 8* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2022; 27,880,807 bytes, 128 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page. Kwartaal 1, lessons 1–30. **Only Deel 1 in hand for LJ8** — the download list's remark that LJ8 Deel 2 is “already held” is contradicted by every record (textbooks.tsv, the staging books.json and the push-state carry Deel 1 only); Deel 2 is treated as not in hand.

**Book 13:** *Reader Rekenen/Economie, Leerjaar 10* (programme reader) — Suriname, MinOWC, November 2022; 1,198,264 bytes, 31 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. A programme reader: learning outcomes, theory summaries and exercises **for teacher and pupil** (its foreword calls it a guide “voor de reken-leerkracht en leerling”); textbooks.tsv files it as a pupil book — its rows here are worded as a programme reader, not a pupil textbook, and its teaching items are carried as the foreword states them.

## Papua New Guinea added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Papua New Guinea’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 files = 28 works, all Papua New Guinea Department of Education (NDoE), “Issued free to schools by the Department of Education”: 8 pupil textbook files (7 works — the JICA/QUIS-ME National Mathematics Textbooks, Grades 1–6, with Grades 3 and 5 in two parts each) and 24 teacher files (21 works — 9 JICA teacher’s manual files, 6 works with Grades 3–5 in two parts each, and 15 Standards Based Curriculum (SBC) teacher guides, Elementary Prep to Grade 12, with separate Advanced and General guides at Grades 11 and 12)** — and placed 503 items (102 from the pupil textbooks, 401 teacher-evidence items from the manuals and guides). The expansion map is wrong on both of its numbers (flag 1 — record-side, reported, not fixed by this merge): it said 6 student + 22 teacher in hand; the true set is 8 student + 24 teacher. The map missed three files that exist only as textbooks.tsv rows (the Grade 4 and Grade 6 full-year JICA pupil textbooks and the Grade 6 JICA manual) and did not know the Grade 2 SBC guide, which has a staging books.json row but no textbooks.tsv row and no push-state entry (flag 4). **Filing error (flag 2 — record-side): the git-tree copies recorded for the Grade 1 and Grade 2 JICA manuals are a different country’s book** — both TSV tree paths hold the identical file, a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual in Portuguese (“Manual do Professor, 1.ª classe”, 5,891,019 B / 216 pp, the same MD5 at both paths — the only byte-identical duplicate pair found in this hunt, and not a PNG book), which also sits in the release `mid-size-books-8` correctly named as a Mozambique book. The genuine PNG manuals were read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books`, whose assets match the staged sizes and checklist MD5s exactly; the push-state’s per-folder landing claims for those two files should be treated as unreliable until the tree paths are re-filed. Flag 3: the two manuals’ recorded education.gov.pg source URLs do not serve the staged pressings — both returned an HTML page during the read, and a later re-probe served a different pressing of the Grade 1 manual — so the staged pressings cannot be re-grabbed from the recorded source URLs. Flag 5: the Grade 9 SBC guide’s textbooks.tsv tree path 404s; the file was recovered by searching all release assets and matches books.json on size and pages. No Maths hides under other subject labels in the PNG records — all 156 staging books.json entries and all 163 textbooks.tsv rows were checked by subject, title and path. Link-only Maths not in hand and not read (it does not count against closure): the FODE distance course-books for Grades 7–12 Mathematics / Maths A / Maths G (unit PDFs) and the two OBE upper-secondary guides found 2026-11-10 (Advanced Mathematics TG, ISBN 978-9980-9924-8-2; General Mathematics TG). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 1 Textbook and its manual print “Subtracation” in the Chapter/Unit 12 title; the SBC guides print “Angels” for Angles in places, “Algebrain” for Algebraic (a Grade 11 guide’s Factorisation unit header) and “Orobability” for Probability (the Grade 9 guide’s strand table); the Grade 1 SBC guide’s Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant. The Grade 3 SBC programme has no Money topic where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error. Printed-page basis: JICA textbook pages cite the book’s own printed folio / contents page; JICA manuals cite the manual’s own printed contents page; SBC guides cite the guide’s printed programme/contents pages (the basis is stated in each book entry below); where a contents extraction was partly two-column-interleaved, the PDF page is given as well and marked “PDF p.”. Counting grain: one item per chapter (pupil textbooks), per unit (teacher’s manuals — the manual unit mirrors the textbook chapter of the same number and is counted once, as teacher evidence), and per named topic/unit in the SBC guides’ own programmes. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the end-of-book/year “Summary” chapters and units in the JICA textbooks and manuals (review apparatus), and the SBC guides’ front sections, assessment-record templates, glossaries/resources and integrated cross-subject project sections. Pupil-textbook rows are COUNTED normally; every manual and SBC guide row is teacher evidence — named in the countries line and in the rows, but outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 20 items — 8 pupil-textbook rows and 12 teacher-evidence rows — from Book S2 (*National Mathematics Textbook Grade 2*), Book S3 (*National Mathematics Textbook Grade 3, Part 1*), Book S4 (*National Mathematics Textbook Grade 3, Part 2*), Book T2 (*Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 2*), Book T3 (*National Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 3, Part 1*), Book T4 (*National Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 3, Part 2*), Book T12 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Two (SBC)*), Book T13 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 3 (SBC)*).

**Book S2:** *National Mathematics Textbook Grade 2* (pupil textbook) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 33,863,464 bytes, 236 PDF pp. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — counted. Printed folio = the book’s contents page; chapter openers verified against the book’s own contents list.

**Book S3:** *National Mathematics Textbook Grade 3, Part 1* (pupil textbook) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME), 2019; 19,009,893 bytes, 118 PDF pp. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — counted. Part 1 = Chapters 1–10. Printed page from the book’s contents (printed = PDF − 10).

**Book S4:** *National Mathematics Textbook Grade 3, Part 2* (pupil textbook) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME), 2019; 14,426,183 bytes, 110 PDF pp. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — counted. Part 2 = Chapters 11–20 (opens at Chapter 11, Large Numbers).

**Book T2:** *Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 2* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 154,818,560 bytes, 400 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — as Book T1. Its 17 units mirror the Grade 2 Textbook’s chapters 1–17; manual printed pages are the manual’s own contents pages. Read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books` copy, MD5-matched to the staged checklist: the textbooks.tsv git-tree path recorded for this manual holds the same Mozambique book as T1’s path (flag 2), and its recorded source URL does not serve this staged pressing (flag 3).

**Book T3:** *National Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 3, Part 1* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 18,104,370 bytes, 178 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the manual guides the Grade 3 Textbook chapter by chapter (lesson flow, objectives, assessment, chapter tests). Part 1 = Units 1–10; manual printed pages are the manual’s own contents pages.

**Book T4:** *National Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 3, Part 2* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 14,847,377 bytes, 170 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — as Book T3. Part 2 = Units 11–20.

**Book T12:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Two* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum, 2015; 8,634,404 bytes, 203 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the topic list is the guide’s own, from its strand Assessment plans (printed pp. 180–185) and term assessment records. This guide has no textbooks.tsv row and no phase3-push-state entry — it is recorded only in the staging books.json (flag 4); it was found and read through the authenticated GitHub route at exactly the recorded size and page count.

**Book T13:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 3* (SBC teacher guide, Junior Primary) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum; 24,810,344 bytes, 140 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Extent: unlike the elementary guides, this guide carries sample guided lessons only (printed pp. 14–124), not a full year of daily plans — the topic list stocked here is the full year’s programme, from the guide’s Yearly Overview (printed pp. 10–13). Noted on the read: this SBC programme has no Money topic, where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error.

## Sweden added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sweden's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 594 PDF pages**, of which **10 carry mathematics**: the national curriculum Lgr22, 2 SPSM pupil materials, and 7 SPSM teacher guides, notes and support publications. Sweden has **no state pupil textbook series** — textbook approval was abolished in 1991, Skolverket sets the curriculum (Lgr22), and the state producer SPSM (Specialpedagogiska skolmyndigheten) publishes special-needs materials — so the pupil teaching in hand is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils, and nothing else: 13 pupil items (the Abakus exercise book and the Växelspelet money game), 81 teacher-evidence items and 79 curriculum-evidence items from Lgr22's Matematik kursplan, 173 items in all. The other 3 files in the read set prove on reading to carry no maths at all and are stocked nowhere: *Skrivmaterial för elever som behöver förstärkta linjer och rutor* is blank ruled/grid stationery (copying masters), the *ABC Skriva-Läsa* teacher guide is literacy only, and a generically named *Arbetsblad* worksheet (pharmacy vocabulary) was pulled in as a hidden-maths spot check and cleared negative. The expansion map is wrong on both axes (flag F4): it listed 8 files as 3 student + 5 teacher, but two of its eight are not Maths at all (F1: the Skriva-Läsa guide, filed in books.json under “Matematik; Svenska”, is a literacy guide — its only number-adjacent moment, drawing a numbered slip 2–5 to pick that many pictures (printed p. 6), is classroom apparatus; F3: the Skrivmaterial, counted as a student Maths book, is blank stationery), a third is the wrong kind (F2: *Räkna med mig*, filed as a student book, is a 105-page handbook for teachers of braille-using pupils — all 35 of its items are teacher evidence), and four maths-bearing files were missed by the map entirely — Lgr22 itself (its section 5.5 is the full Matematik syllabus), the *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* support publication (filed under generic special education), *Växelspelet* (filed under “Verklighetsuppfattning”) and the *Jag handlar* teacher guide (filed under home-economics subjects). Flags F1–F4 are record/map-side: they are reported here and carried in this section; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/sweden.md filing exists. The books' own errors are carried corrected: F5 — both Abakus volumes misprint their print ISBNs with a “987-” prefix (Övningsbok “987-91-28-11602-2”, Handledning “987-91-28-11600-8”; each book's PDF ISBN carries the correct 978- prefix); F6 — *Räkna med mig*'s ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2”; F7 — the Abakus Handledning's division chapter (printed p. 45) says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division, taught corrected on the division row it touches; F8 — a box in *Räkna med mig* (printed p. 38) misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning”. The placements' record items are stocked nowhere: R1 (Lgr22's assessment criteria, printed pp. 60–63 — they restate the central content as standards and add no content beyond the items), R2 (the Abakus Handledning's exercise sets and maths-card copying masters), R3 (the Arbeta publication's overview material), R4 (*Räkna med mig*'s apparatus), R5 (the Diamanterna card game itself is a physical product, not a book, and is not in hand — only its one-page SPSM note is) and R6 (the *Jag handlar* pupil book is recorded only as a sample that was never staged as a secured file; only its teacher guide is in hand). One basis note: the Övningsbok's multiplication and division exercise pages are abacus diagrams with no printed numerals, so those two placements rest on the book's contents page, section headings and introduction, exactly as the placements state. Sweden's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but 160 of its 173 items are teacher or curriculum evidence and its pupil side is special-needs material only. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items: 4 teacher evidence, 2 pupil teaching. Sweden's pupil items in this lesson are genuine pupil teaching, so Sweden is added to this lesson's acceptance count above and to the countries line; its teacher and curriculum rows here are evidence only and carry no count.

**Book 2:** *Räkna med Abakus – Övningsbok* (pupil exercise book) — Sweden, SPSM, 2017, best.nr 11602; 1,647,686 bytes, 58 PDF pp., MD5 d8b1209258a64d21b43b9f2172e47364. Pupil exercise book for the Japanese soroban abacus, produced in braille and print editions mainly for blind pupils; exercises carry their answers printed beside them (self-checking design). PUPIL TEACHING. Book error (flag F5): the print ISBN is misprinted “987-91-28-11602-2” — the PDF ISBN is correctly 978-91-28-11605-3; the error is the book's and the content is taught as printed.

**Book 3:** *Växelspelet* (pupil game rules) — Sweden, SPSM, art. 12800; 1,593,356 bytes, 4 PDF pp., MD5 09c5d0cdd084e777ab56394dd4c49ed4. A pupil game about exchanging Swedish money, cited by its printed pp. 1–4; filed in the records under “Verklighetsuppfattning”, not under Matematik — confirmed on reading as a pupil maths game. PUPIL TEACHING.

**Book 4:** *Räkna med Abakus – Handledning* (teacher guide) — Sweden, SPSM, 2017, art. 11600/11604; 11,921,933 bytes, 60 PDF pp., MD5 aecd2e237b2b59a439adf7ac8db21042. Teacher guide to the same soroban method as Book 2; grabbed from its recorded source_url after the GitHub Contents API returned 404. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Book errors: the print ISBN is misprinted “987-91-28-11600-8” (flag F5; the PDF ISBN is correctly 978-91-28-11604-6), and on printed p. 45 the division chapter says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division (flag F7) — taught corrected on the division row it touches. Its exercise sets (printed pp. 51–53) and maths-card copying masters (printed pp. 55–56) are apparatus — record item R2, stocked nowhere.

**Book 9:** *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* (support publication) — Sweden, SPSM, 2021, ISBN 978-91-28-00992-8; 764,290 bytes, 26 PDF pp., MD5 60b56c2eb928a6983dceccca12e03797. SPSM support publication on the language of mathematics for pupils with developmental language disorder. It teaches no pupil maths directly; the items placed from it are the concrete maths content it names, demonstrates or uses as worked material, placed as teacher evidence. Its overview material — the catalogue of known difficulty points (printed p. 4), its concrete-versus-abstract discussion (p. 9) and its general support catalogue (pp. 15, 17) — is record item R3, stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 10:** *Räkna med mig* (teacher handbook) — Sweden, SPSM, 2018, best.nr 10386; 20,465,920 bytes, 105 PDF pp., MD5 5fbeac506f239001f466a59aa7574d9e. A 105-page handbook on mathematics teaching for compulsory-school pupils who use braille, addressed to teachers (foreword, printed p. 7) and built on the Lgr11 central content; grabbed from its recorded source_url after the GitHub Contents API returned 404. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong (flag F2): it is not a pupil book, and all 35 of its items are teacher evidence. Book errors: its ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2” (flag F6), and a box on printed p. 38 misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning” (flag F8); the Abakus guide placed from Book 4 is the real book. Its apparatus — the accessible-learning-environment and adapted/national tests chapters (printed pp. 14–26), the tools and materials catalogue (pp. 85–91), the maths word list (p. 95), the table of common signs in maths braille (pp. 96–97) and the copying masters (pp. 98–103) — is record item R4, stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

Sweden enters this lesson as pupil teaching on the rows marked pupil teaching above — the Övningsbok and Växelspelet are genuine pupil materials (SPSM special-needs publications) — and is counted in the acceptance count above on those pupil rows alone; every other Sweden row in this lesson is teacher or curriculum evidence and is counted nowhere. Sweden's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-heavy): the in-hand set is read and stocked in full, but there is no mainstream pupil textbook in hand — Sweden has no state pupil textbook series, and its pupil side here is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils only.
